--     [RESIZE] setSize WxH  cellSize=N
--     [RESIZE] stop (saved)
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local QuestsField = NSQC4.QuestsFieldClass
    local CONST       = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not QuestsField or not CONST then return end

    local COLS, ROWS     = CONST.COLS, CONST.ROWS
    local CELL_GAP       = CONST.CELL_GAP
    local GRID_PADDING   = CONST.GRID_PADDING
    local MIN_CELL       = CONST.MIN_CELL
    local MAX_CELL       = CONST.MAX_CELL

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Отладка
    -- ========================================================================
    local ResizeDebug = { DEBUG = false }
    QuestsField._ResizeDebug = ResizeDebug

    local function dbg(...)
        if ResizeDebug.DEBUG then print("|cffff8800[RESIZE]|r", ...) end
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Создание хэндла
    -- ========================================================================
    function QuestsField:CreateResizeHandle()
        local f = self.frame
        local h = CreateFrame("Button", "NSQC4FieldResize", f)
        h:SetSize(16, 16)
        h:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", f, "BOTTOMRIGHT", 4, -4)
        h:SetFrameLevel(f:GetFrameLevel() + 30)
        h:SetNormalTexture("Interface\\ChatFrame\\UI-ChatIM-SizeGrabber-Up")
        h:SetPushedTexture("Interface\\ChatFrame\\UI-ChatIM-SizeGrabber-Down")
        h:SetHighlightTexture("Interface\\ChatFrame\\UI-ChatIM-SizeGrabber-Highlight")
        h:EnableMouse(true)

        local anchorL, anchorT
        local lastCell
        local inst = self

        -- ====================================================================
        -- apply — вычислить новый cellSize по позиции мыши, обрезать по eff,
        -- применить через ApplyCellSize (или вручную, если метода нет).
        -- ====================================================================
        local function apply()
            local scale = UIParent:GetEffectiveScale()
            local cx, cy = GetCursorPosition()
            cx, cy = cx / scale, cy / scale

            local rawW = cx - anchorL
            local rawH = anchorT - cy

            local csX = (rawW - (COLS - 1) * CELL_GAP - GRID_PADDING * 2) / COLS
            local csY = (rawH - (ROWS - 1) * CELL_GAP - GRID_PADDING * 2) / ROWS
            local cs  = math.max(csX, csY)
            cs = math.floor(cs + 0.5)

            local beforeMin = cs
            if cs < MIN_CELL then cs = MIN_CELL end

            -- effMaxCell — из MaxCellForScreen (Frame.lua), fallback MAX_CELL
            local effMaxCell, maxFromScreen
            if inst.MaxCellForScreen then
                effMaxCell, maxFromScreen = inst:MaxCellForScreen()
            else
                effMaxCell    = MAX_CELL
                maxFromScreen = MAX_CELL
            end

            local beforeClamp = cs
            if cs > effMaxCell then cs = effMaxCell end

            dbg(string.format(
                "apply  csX=%.1f csY=%.1f raw=%.1f → min=%d → clamp=%d  eff=%d fromScreen=%d  frameW=%.1f",
                csX, csY, beforeMin, cs, beforeClamp, effMaxCell, maxFromScreen,
                f:GetWidth()))

            if cs == lastCell then return end
            lastCell = cs

            if inst.ApplyCellSize then
                inst:ApplyCellSize(cs)
            else
                inst.cellSize = cs
                local w, hh = inst._FrameSizeForCell(cs)
                f:SetSize(w, hh)
                inst:ReflowCells()
                inst:ApplyBorderAnchors()
                if inst.RefreshAllAnchorSizes then inst:RefreshAllAnchorSizes() end
                local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
                if Drops and Drops.RefreshAllLootSizes then Drops:RefreshAllLootSizes() end
            end

            dbg(string.format("setSize  %.1fx%.1f  cellSize=%d", f:GetWidth(), f:GetHeight(), cs))
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- OnMouseDown: старт ресайза
        -- ====================================================================
        h:SetScript("OnMouseDown", function(self2, button)
            if button ~= "LeftButton" then return end
            anchorL = f:GetLeft()
            anchorT = f:GetTop()
            lastCell = nil
            dbg(string.format("start  anchorL=%.1f anchorT=%.1f frameW=%.1f cellSize=%s",
                anchorL or -1, anchorT or -1, f:GetWidth(), tostring(inst.cellSize)))

            self2:SetScript("OnUpdate", function()
                if not IsMouseButtonDown("LeftButton") then
                    self2:SetScript("OnUpdate", nil)
                    inst:Save()
                    dbg("stop (saved)")
                    return
                end
                apply()
            end)
            apply()
        end)

        h:SetScript("OnHide", function(self2) self2:SetScript("OnUpdate", nil) end)

        self.resizeHandle = h
        return h
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Слэш-команда: /ns_resize on | off | dump
    -- ========================================================================
    SLASH_NSRESIZE1 = "/ns_resize"
    SlashCmdList["NSRESIZE"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "on" then
            ResizeDebug.DEBUG = true
            print("[RESIZE] DEBUG = true")
            local F = NSQC4.QuestsField
            if F and F.MaxCellForScreen then
                local e, m = F:MaxCellForScreen()
                print(string.format("[RESIZE] eff=%d fromScreen=%d MAX_CELL=%d MIN_CELL=%d",
                    e, m, MAX_CELL, MIN_CELL))
            end
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            ResizeDebug.DEBUG = false
            print("[RESIZE] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "dump" then
            local F = NSQC4.QuestsField
            local e, m = F:MaxCellForScreen()
            print(string.format("[RESIZE] dump: cellSize=%s frameW=%s eff=%d fromScreen=%d",
                tostring(F.cellSize),
                tostring(F.frame and F.frame:GetWidth() or "nil"),
                e, m))
        else
            print("[RESIZE] /ns_resize on | off | dump")
        end
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Behavior/Init.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Behavior / Init
-- Реестр поведений по видам и характерам.
--
--   NSQC4.QuestsDfBehavior[kind][behavior] = { Tick = function(entity) ... end }
--
-- kind     — "goblin" | "dwarf" | ...
-- behavior — "aggressive" | "peaceful" | ...
--
-- Каждый вид регистрирует свои поведения в df/<Kind>/Behavior/*.lua.
-- Если чего-то нет — Tick упадёт в Default.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Registry = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfBehavior = Registry

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Регистрация поведения.
    --   kind     — "goblin" | "dwarf"
    --   behavior — "aggressive" | "peaceful"
    --   handler  — { Tick = function(entity) ... end }
    -- ========================================================================
    function Registry:Register(kind, behavior, handler)
        if type(kind) ~= "string" or kind == "" then return false end
        if type(behavior) ~= "string" or behavior == "" then return false end
        if type(handler) ~= "table" then return false end

        if not self[kind] then self[kind] = {} end
        self[kind][behavior] = handler
        return true
    end

    function Registry:Get(kind, behavior)
        if type(kind) ~= "string" or type(behavior) ~= "string" then return nil end
        local byKind = self[kind]
        return byKind and byKind[behavior] or nil
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Complete.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Complete
-- Режим (build/demolish) определяется по:
--   wasBuilding = ai.wasBuilding (ставится в Work при DONE)
--   + terrainId = ai.terrainId ("ladder" для стройки/лома)
-- ai.workMode НЕ используется (в Work его больше нет).
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Complete = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIComplete = Complete

    local Grid      = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    local Transform = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransform
    local State     = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIState
    local ProfApi   = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsApi

    local PROF_MAX   = 100
    local CHANCE_MAX = 100

    local TERRAIN_TO_PROF = {
        earth  = "digger",
        tree   = "lumberjack",
        stone  = "stonecutter",
        grass  = "herbalist",
        ladder = "builder",
    }

    local PROF_GOD = {
        digger      = 1,
        lumberjack  = 2,
        herbalist   = 2,
        stonecutter = 1,
        builder     = 1,
    }

    Complete.DEBUG = true

    local dbg = {
        calls    = 0, skipped  = 0, maxed    = 0, learned  = 0,
        rolls    = 0, success  = 0, up       = 0, learn    = 0,
        miss     = 0, noslot   = 0, max      = 0,
        startedAt = GetTime(),
    }
    Complete._dbg = dbg

    local function PrintStats(tag)
        local elapsed = GetTime() - dbg.startedAt
        local rate = (dbg.rolls > 0) and (dbg.success / dbg.rolls * 100) or 0
        print(string.format(
            "[COMPLETE %s] t=%ds calls=%d skip=%d rolls=%d succ=%d rate=%.1f%% | up=%d learn=%d miss=%d noslot=%d max=%d",
            tag or "", math.floor(elapsed),
            dbg.calls, dbg.skipped, dbg.rolls, dbg.success, rate,
            dbg.up, dbg.learn, dbg.miss, dbg.noslot, dbg.max))
    end

    function Complete:ResetStats()
        dbg.calls=0; dbg.skipped=0; dbg.maxed=0; dbg.learned=0
        dbg.rolls=0; dbg.success=0; dbg.up=0; dbg.learn=0
        dbg.miss=0; dbg.noslot=0; dbg.max=0
        dbg.startedAt = GetTime()
        print("[COMPLETE] статистика сброшена.")
    end

    function Complete:DumpStats() PrintStats("dump") end

    local function CalcChance(int, kinship, godBit)
        int     = tonumber(int) or 0
        kinship = tonumber(kinship) or 0
        godBit  = tonumber(godBit) or 0
        if int <= 0 or godBit <= 0 then return 0, 1, "none" end
        local hasGod = (math.floor(kinship / godBit) % 2 == 1)
        if not hasGod then return math.min(CHANCE_MAX, int * 1), 1, "none" end
        if kinship == godBit then return math.min(CHANCE_MAX, int * 3), 3, "single" end
        return math.min(CHANCE_MAX, int * 2), 2, "mixed"
    end

    local function LineStr(mult, line)
        if line == "single" then return "x3 single"
        elseif line == "mixed" then return "x2 mixed"
        else return "x1 none" end
    end

    local function PrintSkillLine(entity, terrainId, profId)
        if not Complete.DEBUG then return end
        local opts = entity and entity.opts or {}
        local sk = {}
        for n, l in pairs(opts.known_skills or {}) do
            sk[#sk + 1] = tostring(n) .. ":" .. tostring(l)
        end
        table.sort(sk)
        print(string.format(
            "[COMPLETE skill] %s | int=%s kin=%s gods=%s | skills_max=%s/%s known=[%s] | terrain=%s profId=%s",
            tostring(opts.name or (entity and entity.id) or "?"),
            tostring(opts.intellect), tostring(opts.kinship),
            (opts.gods and table.concat(opts.gods, ",")) or "-",
            tostring(opts.skills_max), tostring(opts.max_skills_max),
            table.concat(sk, ", "), tostring(terrainId), tostring(profId)))
    end

    local function TryUpProfession(entity, terrainId)
        dbg.calls = dbg.calls + 1
        if not entity or not entity.opts then dbg.skipped = dbg.skipped + 1; return nil, nil, nil, "skip" end
        local opts = entity.opts
        local int = tonumber(opts.intellect) or 0
        if int <= 0 then dbg.skipped = dbg.skipped + 1; return nil, nil, nil, "skip" end

        local profId = terrainId and TERRAIN_TO_PROF[terrainId] or nil
        if not profId then dbg.skipped = dbg.skipped + 1; return nil, nil, nil, "noprof" end
        local profDef = ProfApi and ProfApi:Get(profId) or nil
        if not profDef or not profDef.name then dbg.skipped = dbg.skipped + 1; return nil, nil, nil, "noprof" end

        local skills = opts.known_skills
        if type(skills) ~= "table" then skills = {}; opts.known_skills = skills end

        local name   = profDef.name
        local cur    = tonumber(skills[name]) or 0
        local kinship = tonumber(opts.kinship) or 0
        local godBit  = PROF_GOD[profId] or 0
        local chancePct, mult, line = CalcChance(int, kinship, godBit)

        if cur > 0 then
            if cur >= PROF_MAX then
                dbg.maxed = dbg.maxed + 1; dbg.max = dbg.max + 1
                return name, cur, cur, "max", nil, int, chancePct, mult, line
            end
            local roll = math.random(1, 100)
            dbg.rolls = dbg.rolls + 1
            if roll <= chancePct then
                skills[name] = cur + 1
                dbg.success = dbg.success + 1; dbg.up = dbg.up + 1
                return name, cur, cur + 1, "up", roll, int, chancePct, mult, line
            end
            dbg.miss = dbg.miss + 1
            return name, cur, cur, "miss", roll, int, chancePct, mult, line
        end

        local smax    = tonumber(opts.skills_max)     or 0
        local smaxmax = tonumber(opts.max_skills_max) or smax
        if smax >= smaxmax then
            dbg.maxed = dbg.maxed + 1; dbg.noslot = dbg.noslot + 1
            return name, 0, 0, "noslot", nil, int, chancePct, mult, line,
                string.format("skills_max=%d/%d", smax, smaxmax)
        end

        local roll = math.random(1, 100)
        dbg.rolls = dbg.rolls + 1
        if roll <= chancePct then
            skills[name] = 1
            opts.skills_max = smax + 1
            dbg.learned = dbg.learned + 1; dbg.learn = dbg.learn + 1
            dbg.success = dbg.success + 1
            return name, 0, 1, "learn", roll, int, chancePct, mult, line,
                string.format("skills_max %d→%d", smax, smax + 1)
        end
        dbg.miss = dbg.miss + 1
        return name, 0, 0, "learnmiss", roll, int, chancePct, mult, line
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Finish
    -- ========================================================================
    function Complete:Finish(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end

        local ai = entity.opts.ai
        if not ai or not ai.target then return false end

        local target     = ai.target
        local workTarget = ai.workTarget or ai.target
        local terrainId  = ai.terrainId
        local profId     = ai.profId or TERRAIN_TO_PROF[terrainId]

        local wasBuilding = (ai.wasBuilding == true)
        ai.wasBuilding = nil

        local profDef = profId and ProfApi and ProfApi:Get(profId) or nil
        local yield   = profDef and profDef.work and profDef.work.yields or nil

        PrintSkillLine(entity, terrainId, profId)

        local pname, pold, pnew, result, proll, pint, ppct, pmult, pline, extra =
            TryUpProfession(entity, terrainId)

        local profStr = ""
        if pname then
            local ch = string.format("p=%d%% %s", ppct or 0, LineStr(pmult, pline))
            if result == "up" then
                profStr = string.format(" | ПРОФА %s %d→%d (%s roll=%d/100 int=%d) UP",
                    pname, pold, pnew, ch, proll, pint)
            elseif result == "learn" then
                profStr = string.format(" | ПРОФА %s ВЫУЧЕНА 0→1 (%s roll=%d/100 int=%d) [%s] LEARN",
                    pname, ch, proll, pint, extra or "")
            elseif result == "miss" then
                profStr = string.format(" | ПРОФА %s miss (%s roll=%d/100 int=%d)",
                    pname, ch, proll, pint)
            elseif result == "learnmiss" then
                profStr = string.format(" | ПРОФА %s не выучена (%s roll=%d/100 int=%d)",
                    pname, ch, proll, pint)
            elseif result == "noslot" then
                profStr = string.format(" | ПРОФА %s нет слота (%s) [%s]",
                    pname, ch, extra or "skills_max=max")
            elseif result == "max" then
                profStr = string.format(" | ПРОФА %s=max(100) (шанс был %d%% %s)",
                    pname, ppct or 0, LineStr(pmult, pline))
            end
        elseif result == "noprof" then
            profStr = string.format(" | ПРОФА нет для terrain=%s", tostring(terrainId))
        else
            profStr = " | ПРОФА skip"
        end

        print(string.format("[NSQC4] %s закончил с %s (клетка %d)%s%s",
            entity.opts.name or "Существо",
            terrainId or "?", workTarget or target,
            yield and (" → " .. yield) or "", profStr))

        if Complete.DEBUG then PrintStats("now") end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- BUILD / DEMOLISH: режим — по wasBuilding + terrainId.
        -- workMode не используется.
        -- ====================================================================
        if wasBuilding and Grid and Grid.IsBuilding then
            if terrainId == "ladder" then
                -- как отличить build от demolish? По текущему hp:
                --   hp >= max → build (достроено)
                --   hp <= 0   → demolish (сломано)
                local b = Grid:GetBuilding(workTarget)
                local hp  = b and tonumber(b.hp)  or 0
                local max = b and tonumber(b.max) or 0

                if max > 0 and hp >= max then
                    print(string.format(
                        "|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Постройка завершена (клетка %d), лестница готова.",
                        workTarget))
                elseif hp <= 0 then
                    print(string.format(
                        "|cffff8800[NSQC4]|r Лестница сломана (клетка %d).", workTarget))
                    local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
                    if Terrain and Terrain.SetTerrain then
                        Terrain:SetTerrain(workTarget, "void")
                    end
                else
                    -- неполное: не должно происходить (DONE по hp)
                    print(string.format(
                        "|cffffaa00[NSQC4]|r Лестница в клетке %d: hp=%d/%d (непонятное состояние).",
                        workTarget, hp, max))
                end
                if State and State.SetIdle then State:SetIdle(entity) end
                return true
            end
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- ОБЫЧНАЯ РАБОТА
        -- ====================================================================
        if wasBuilding then
            print(string.format(
                "|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Постройка завершена (клетка %d), лестница готова.",
                workTarget))
        else
            local isUnfinishedBuilding = (terrainId == "ladder")
                and Grid and Grid.IsBuilding and Grid:IsBuilding(workTarget)
            if isUnfinishedBuilding then
                print(string.format(
                    "|cffffaa00[NSQC4]|r Постройка приостановлена (клетка %d): не хватает материала.",
                    workTarget))
            else
                if Transform and Transform.Apply then
                    Transform:Apply(target, terrainId)
                elseif Grid then
                    Grid:SetHardness(target, 0)
                end
            end
        end

        if State and State.SetIdle then State:SetIdle(entity) end
        return true
    end

    SLASH_NSCOMPLETE1 = "/ns_complete"
    SlashCmdList["NSCOMPLETE"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "dump" then
            Complete:DumpStats()
        elseif cmd == "on" then
            Complete.DEBUG = true
            print("[COMPLETE] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            Complete.DEBUG = false
            print("[COMPLETE] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "reset" then
            Complete:ResetStats()
        else
            print("[COMPLETE] /ns_complete on | off | dump | reset")
        end
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Default.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Default
-- Поведение по умолчанию.
--
-- НОВАЯ МОДЕЛЬ ДВИЖЕНИЯ (см. Move.lua):
--   entity.index      — клетка, где существо ЧИСЛИТСЯ
--   entity.nextIndex  — клетка, к центру которой идёт
--   entity.posX/posY  — реальная позиция (пиксели)
--   Move:Advance возвращает:
--     "moving"  — существо между центрами, ещё не дошло до nextIndex
--     "moved"   — дошло до центра промежуточной клетки, идёт дальше
--     "arrived" — дошло до центра последней клетки пути
--     "wait"    — ждём nextStepAt
--     nil       — ошибка/блокировка
--
-- Цикл стройки (builder):
--   idle → Pick(лестница) → идём к ближайшему материалу →
--   взяли 1 ед. в ai.carrying → идём к лестнице →
--   положили в клетку (Grid:AddStored) → строим порциями по 100 HP →
--   материал кончился → need_material → снова к материалу → … → DONE.
--
-- ОТЛАДКА:
--   /ns_def on | off | dump [name]
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Default = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIDefault = Default
    Default.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if Default.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[DEF]|r", ...) end
    end

    local State    = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIState
    local Find     = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIFind
    local Path     = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIPath
    local Move     = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIMove
    local Work     = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIWork
    local Complete = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIComplete

    if not (State and Find and Path and Move and Work and Complete) then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Default: не все зависимости загружены.")
        return
    end

    local function PlayStartSound(entity)
        local Sound = NSQC4.QuestsDfAISound
        if Sound then
            Sound:PlayKind(entity.kind or (entity.opts and entity.opts.kind))
        end
    end

    local function DiagnosePath(entity, targetIndex)
        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return end
        local c = Grid:GetCell(targetIndex)
        if not c then return end
        dbg(string.format("  target %s: terrain=%s entityName=%s passable(raw)=%s passable(ent)=%s",
            tostring(targetIndex), tostring(c.terrain), tostring(c.entityName),
            Grid:IsPassable(targetIndex) and "yes" or "no",
            Grid:IsPassable(targetIndex, entity) and "yes" or "no"))
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Ближайшая клетка с материалом (по BFS-длине).
    -- ========================================================================
    local function FindClosestMaterialCell(entity, material)
        local Grid  = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        if not Grid or not Drops then return nil end
        local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
        local TOTAL = CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS

        local bestIdx, bestLen = nil, math.huge
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            if not Grid:IsReserved(i, entity.id) then
                local items = Drops:GetAll(i)
                for _, d in ipairs(items) do
                    if d.id == material then
                        local path = Path:Find(entity.index, i, entity)
                        if path and #path < bestLen then
                            bestIdx, bestLen = i, #path
                        end
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return bestIdx
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Проверка «цель ещё в задачах» — по workTarget.
    -- ========================================================================
    local function IsTargetStillSelected(entity)
        local ai = entity and entity.opts and entity.opts.ai
        if not ai then return false end
        local checkIdx = ai.workTarget or ai.target
        if not checkIdx or not ai.terrainId then return false end
        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return true end
        return Grid:IsTerrainSelected(checkIdx, ai.terrainId) and true or false
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Обработка прибытия (когда Move вернул "arrived").
    -- ========================================================================
    local function OnArrived(entity, ai, name)
        local Grid  = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        local bIdx  = ai.workTarget or ai.target

        -- ====================================================================
        -- BUILDER
        -- ====================================================================
        if ai.profId == "builder" then
            -- пришли к материалу (target != workTarget)
            if ai.target ~= bIdx then
                local hereItems = Drops and Drops:GetAll(ai.target) or {}
                local took = nil
                for _, d in ipairs(hereItems) do
                    local b = Grid:GetBuilding(bIdx)
                    local material = b and b.material or "wood"
                    if d.id == material then
                        local c = Grid:GetCell(ai.target)
                        if c and c.loot and c.loot[d.anchor] then
                            c.loot[d.anchor] = nil
                            local any = false
                            for _ in pairs(c.loot) do any = true; break end
                            if not any then c.loot = nil end
                            Drops:Render(ai.target)
                            State:SetCarrying(entity, material, 1)
                            took = material
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
                if took then
                    local path = Path:Find(entity.index, bIdx, entity)
                    if not path then State:SetIdle(entity); return end
                    local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, bIdx, "ladder", "builder", path,
                        { workTarget = bIdx })
                    if not ok then State:SetIdle(entity); return end
                    dbg(string.format("%s took material %s → build %s",
                        tostring(name), tostring(took), tostring(bIdx)))
                    return
                end
            end

            -- пришли к лестнице: если несём материал — кладём
            if ai.carrying then
                local carry = ai.carrying
                Grid:AddStored(bIdx, carry.count or 1)
                ai.carrying = nil
                dbg(string.format("%s deposited %s → cell %s stored=%d",
                    tostring(name), tostring(carry.material), tostring(bIdx),
                    Grid:GetStored(bIdx)))
                local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi
                if T and T.RenderCellFromStorage then T:RenderCellFromStorage(bIdx) end
            end

            State:SetWorking(entity)
            return
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- Обычная работа
        -- ====================================================================
        if not ai.carrying then
            local hereItems = Drops and Drops:GetAll(ai.target) or {}
            local took = nil
            for _, d in ipairs(hereItems) do
                local b = Grid:GetBuilding(bIdx)
                local material = b and b.material or "wood"
                if d.id == material then
                    local c = Grid:GetCell(ai.target)
                    if c and c.loot and c.loot[d.anchor] then
                        c.loot[d.anchor] = nil
                        local any = false
                        for _ in pairs(c.loot) do any = true; break end
                        if not any then c.loot = nil end
                        Drops:Render(ai.target)
                        State:SetCarrying(entity, material, 1)
                        took = material
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
            if took then
                local path = Path:Find(entity.index, bIdx, entity)
                if not path then State:SetIdle(entity); return end
                local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, bIdx, "ladder", "builder", path,
                    { workTarget = bIdx })
                if not ok then State:SetIdle(entity); return end
                return
            end
        end

        State:SetWorking(entity)
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Tick
    -- ========================================================================
    function Default:Tick(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end
        if entity.opts.sleeping then return false end

        local ai   = entity.opts.ai
        local name = entity.opts.name or entity.id

        -- снято выделение?
        if ai and ai.target and (ai.state == "moving" or ai.state == "working") then
            if not IsTargetStillSelected(entity) then
                dbg(string.format("%s: %s (terrain=%s) снято выделение → idle",
                    tostring(name), tostring(ai.workTarget or ai.target),
                    tostring(ai.terrainId)))
                State:SetIdle(entity)
                return true
            end
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- IDLE → выбор цели
        -- ====================================================================
        if State:IsIdle(entity) then
            local pick = Find:Pick(entity)
            if not pick then return false end

            if pick.demolish then
                local path = Path:Find(entity.index, pick.index, entity)
                if not path then DiagnosePath(entity, pick.index); return false end
                local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, pick.index, "ladder", nil, path,
                    { workTarget = pick.index })
                if not ok then return false end
                PlayStartSound(entity)
                return true
            end

            if pick.profId == "builder" then
                local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
                local bIdx = pick.index
                local stored = Grid.GetStored and Grid:GetStored(bIdx) or 0
                local carry = ai and ai.carrying

                -- 1. уже несём → сразу к лестнице
                if carry then
                    local path = Path:Find(entity.index, bIdx, entity)
                    if not path then DiagnosePath(entity, bIdx); return false end
                    local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, bIdx, "ladder", "builder", path,
                        { workTarget = bIdx })
                    if not ok then return false end
                    PlayStartSound(entity)
                    return true
                end

                -- 2. в клетке лестницы уже есть материал → строим
                if stored > 0 then
                    local path = Path:Find(entity.index, bIdx, entity)
                    if not path then DiagnosePath(entity, bIdx); return false end
                    local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, bIdx, "ladder", "builder", path,
                        { workTarget = bIdx })
                    if not ok then return false end
                    PlayStartSound(entity)
                    return true
                end

                -- 3. материала нет → к ближайшему
                local b = Grid:GetBuilding(bIdx)
                local material = b and b.material or "wood"
                local matIdx = FindClosestMaterialCell(entity, material)
                if not matIdx then
                    dbg(string.format("%s build: нет материала %s → idle",
                        tostring(name), tostring(material)))
                    return false
                end

                local path = Path:Find(entity.index, matIdx, entity)
                if not path then DiagnosePath(entity, matIdx); return false end
                local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, matIdx, "ladder", "builder", path,
                    { workTarget = bIdx, material = material })
                if not ok then return false end
                dbg(string.format("%s idle→moving material cell=%s → build cell=%s",
                    tostring(name), tostring(matIdx), tostring(bIdx)))
                PlayStartSound(entity)
                return true
            end

            -- обычная работа
            local path = Path:Find(entity.index, pick.index, entity)
            if not path then DiagnosePath(entity, pick.index); return false end
            local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, pick.index, pick.terrainId, pick.profId, path, nil)
            if not ok then return false end
            PlayStartSound(entity)
            return true
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- MOVING — четыре возможных исхода
        -- ====================================================================
        if State:IsMoving(entity) then
            local r = Move:Advance(entity)

            if r == "arrived" then
                -- дошли до центра последней клетки
                OnArrived(entity, ai, name)
                return true

            elseif r == "moved" then
                -- дошли до центра промежуточной — продолжаем (следующий тик)
                return true

            elseif r == "moving" then
                -- между центрами, ещё в пути
                return true

            elseif r == "wait" then
                -- ждём nextStepAt
                return true

            else
                -- nil / ошибка / блокировка
                dbg(string.format("%s moving: Move=%s → idle", tostring(name), tostring(r)))
                State:SetIdle(entity)
                return false
            end
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- WORKING
        -- ====================================================================
        if State:IsWorking(entity) then
            local done, reason = Work:Tick(entity)
            if done == true then
                Complete:Finish(entity)
                return true
            elseif done == false then
                if reason == "need_material" then
                    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
                    local bIdx = ai.workTarget or ai.target
                    local b = Grid:GetBuilding(bIdx)
                    local material = b and b.material or "wood"
                    local matIdx = FindClosestMaterialCell(entity, material)
                    if not matIdx then
                        dbg(string.format("%s need_material: нет %s → idle",
                            tostring(name), tostring(material)))
                        State:SetIdle(entity)
                        return false
                    end
                    local path = Path:Find(entity.index, matIdx, entity)
                    if not path then State:SetIdle(entity); return false end
                    local ok = State:SetMoving(entity, matIdx, "ladder", "builder", path,
                        { workTarget = bIdx, material = material })
                    if not ok then State:SetIdle(entity); return false end
                    dbg(string.format("%s working→moving material cell=%s → build cell=%s",
                        tostring(name), tostring(matIdx), tostring(bIdx)))
                    return true
                end
                return true
            else
                State:SetIdle(entity); return false
            end
        end

        State:SetIdle(entity); return false
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- /ns_def
    -- ========================================================================
    SLASH_NSDEF1 = "/ns_def"
    SlashCmdList["NSDEF"] = function(msg)
        local cmd, arg = (msg or ""):match("^(%S*)%s*(.-)$")
        cmd = (cmd or ""):lower()

        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then
            Default.DEBUG = true
            print("[DEF] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            Default.DEBUG = false
            print("[DEF] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "dump" then
            local name = (arg ~= "" and arg) or nil
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if not Entities then return end
            local list = name and { Entities:GetByName(name) } or Entities:GetAll()
            for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                if e then
                    local ai = e.opts and e.opts.ai
                    print(string.format("[DEF] %s idx=%s next=%s pos=(%.1f,%.1f) state=%s target=%s wt=%s terrain=%s carrying=%s pathIdx=%s/#%s",
                        tostring(e.opts and e.opts.name or e.id),
                        tostring(e.index), tostring(e.nextIndex),
                        e.posX or -1, e.posY or -1,
                        tostring(ai and ai.state),
                        tostring(ai and ai.target),
                        tostring(ai and ai.workTarget),
                        tostring(ai and ai.terrainId),
                        tostring(ai and ai.carrying and ai.carrying.material),
                        tostring(ai and ai.pathIdx),
                        tostring(ai and ai.path and #ai.path)))
                end
            end
        else
            print("[DEF] /ns_def on | off | dump [name]")
        end
    end

    print("|cff00ffff[DEF]|r AI/Default загружен (debug=" .. tostring(Default.DEBUG) .. ")")
end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Find.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Find
-- Выбор цели для существа.
--
-- ОТЛАДКА:
--   /ns_find on | off | dump
--   Find.DEBUG = true/false
--
--   При DEBUG=true каждый Pick печатает:
--     [FIND] =========================================================
--     [FIND] Pick entity=NAME kind=goblin canSwim=false
--     [FIND] ladder tasks: N
--     [FIND]   ladder 12 selected=yes hp=500/1000 mode=build by=nil
--     [FIND]   ladder 12 → task build (material=wood)
--     [FIND] selected cells: N
--     [FIND]   sel cell=19 terrain=earth fresh=yes allowed=yes reserved=no
--     [FIND]   sel cell=19 prof=digger → best
--     [FIND]   sel cell=21 terrain=tree fresh=yes allowed=yes reserved=no
--     [FIND]   sel cell=21 prof=nil (нет профессии) → rest
--     [FIND] pool: best=1 rest=0
--     [FIND] pick candidate cell=19 profId=digger → Path:Find...
--     [FIND]   path OK len=5 [74,84,94,95,19]
--     [FIND] → PICK cell=19 terrainId=earth profId=digger demolish=false
--     [FIND] → NO PICK (все недостижимы)
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Find = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIFind = Find
    Find.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if Find.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[FIND]|r", ...) end
    end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Find: Grid не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not CONST then return end
    local TOTAL = CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Allowed по виду
    -- ========================================================================
    local function GetAllowed(kind)
        if kind == "goblin" then
            local g = NSQC4.QuestsDfGoblin
            return g and g.Allowed or nil
        elseif kind == "dwarf" then
            local d = NSQC4.QuestsDfDwarf
            return d and d.Allowed or nil
        end
        return nil
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Выделенные клетки из nsDbc4.quests.df.selections.terrain_types
    -- ========================================================================
    local function GetSelectedCells()
        local result = {}
        nsDbc4 = nsDbc4 or {}
        nsDbc4.quests = nsDbc4.quests or {}
        nsDbc4.quests.df = nsDbc4.quests.df or {}
        nsDbc4.quests.df.selections = nsDbc4.quests.df.selections or {}
        local s = nsDbc4.quests.df.selections
        local byType = s.terrain_types or {}
        for terrainId, list in pairs(byType) do
            for index in pairs(list) do
                result[#result + 1] = { index = index, terrainId = terrainId }
            end
        end
        return result
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Профессия под terrain
    -- ========================================================================
    local function FindProfForTerrain(entity, terrainId)
        local ProfApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsApi
        if not ProfApi then return nil end
        local opts = entity.opts
        if not opts or type(opts.known_skills) ~= "table" then return nil end
        for name, lvl in pairs(opts.known_skills) do
            local def = ProfApi:GetByName(name)
            if def and def.terrain == terrainId then
                return def
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function HasBuilder(entity)
        return FindProfForTerrain(entity, "ladder") ~= nil
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Свежесть выделенной клетки: terrain в Grid совпадает с terrainId в selection
    -- ========================================================================
    local function IsCellFresh(cell)
        if not cell then return false end
        local actual = Grid:GetTerrain(cell.index)
        if not actual then return false end
        if actual == "void" then return false end
        if actual ~= cell.terrainId then return false end
        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Проверка достижимости: Path:Find от текущей позиции до цели.
    -- Возвращает path | nil
    -- ========================================================================
    local function Reachable(entity, targetIndex)
        local Path = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIPath
        if not Path or not Path.Find then return nil end
        return Path:Find(entity.index, targetIndex, entity)
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Задачи по лестнице. Только ВЫДЕЛЕННЫЕ.
    --   hp < max  → build (только если есть builder)
    --   hp >= max → demolish (любой)
    -- ========================================================================
    local function GetLadderTasks(entity)
        local result = {}
        local b = Grid.GetAllBuildings and Grid:GetAllBuildings() or {}
        dbg(string.format("ladder tasks scan: %d buildings", (function()
            local n = 0
            for _ in pairs(b) do n = n + 1 end
            return n
        end)()))

        for idx, rec in pairs(b) do
            if rec then
                local selected = Grid:IsTerrainSelected(idx, "ladder")
                local hp  = tonumber(rec.hp)  or 0
                local max = tonumber(rec.max) or 0

                if not selected then
                    dbg(string.format("  ladder %d selected=no hp=%s/%s → skip",
                        idx, hp, max))
                elseif rec.by and rec.by ~= entity.id then
                    dbg(string.format("  ladder %d selected=yes hp=%s/%s by=%s → skip (занята)",
                        idx, hp, max, tostring(rec.by)))
                elseif hp < max then
                    local hasB = HasBuilder(entity)
                    dbg(string.format("  ladder %d selected=yes hp=%s/%s mat=%s → task build (hasBuilder=%s)",
                        idx, hp, max, tostring(rec.material), tostring(hasB)))
                    if hasB then
                        result[#result + 1] = { index = idx, mode = "build", material = rec.material }
                    end
                elseif hp > 0 then
                    dbg(string.format("  ladder %d selected=yes hp=%s/%s → task demolish", idx, hp, max))
                    result[#result + 1] = { index = idx, mode = "demolish" }
                else
                    dbg(string.format("  ladder %d selected=yes hp=%s/%s → skip (hp<=0)", idx, hp, max))
                end
            end
        end
        return result
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- HasProfForSelected — используется Ticker'ом для приоритета.
    -- Проверяет: есть ли у существа хоть одна задача, которую он МОЖЕТ взять
    -- (без проверки достижимости — это дорого, а Ticker зовёт часто).
    -- ========================================================================
    function Find:HasProfForSelected(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end

        local tasks = GetLadderTasks(entity)
        for _, t in ipairs(tasks) do
            if t.mode == "demolish" then return true end
            if t.mode == "build" and HasBuilder(entity) then return true end
        end

        local kind = entity.kind or entity.opts.kind
        local allowed = GetAllowed(kind)
        if not allowed then return false end

        local selected = GetSelectedCells()
        for _, cell in ipairs(selected) do
            if IsCellFresh(cell) and allowed[cell.terrainId] then
                if FindProfForTerrain(entity, cell.terrainId) then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Pick — основной выбор.
    -- ========================================================================
    function Find:Pick(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return nil end

        local eName = entity.opts.name or entity.id
        local kind  = entity.kind or entity.opts.kind

        -- canSwim (для диагностики)
        local Resolve = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsResolve
        local canSwim = false
        if Resolve and Resolve.CanSwim then
            canSwim = Resolve:CanSwim(entity) and true or false
        end

        dbg("=========================================================")
        dbg(string.format("Pick entity=%s kind=%s idx=%s canSwim=%s",
            tostring(eName), tostring(kind), tostring(entity.index), tostring(canSwim)))

        -- ====================================================================
        -- 1. Задачи по лестнице
        -- ====================================================================
        local tasks = GetLadderTasks(entity)
        dbg(string.format("ladder tasks: %d", #tasks))

        if #tasks > 0 then
            local demolish, build = {}, {}
            for _, t in ipairs(tasks) do
                if t.mode == "demolish" then
                    demolish[#demolish + 1] = t
                elseif t.mode == "build" and HasBuilder(entity) then
                    build[#build + 1] = t
                end
            end

            dbg(string.format("  pools: demolish=%d build=%d", #demolish, #build))

            local pool = (#demolish > 0) and demolish or (#build > 0 and build or nil)
            local mode = (#demolish > 0) and "demolish" or "build"

            if pool and #pool > 0 then
                -- Проверяем достижимость по очереди (начиная со случайного)
                local order = {}
                for i = 1, #pool do order[i] = i end
                for i = #order, 2, -1 do
                    local j = math.random(i)
                    order[i], order[j] = order[j], order[i]
                end

                for _, oi in ipairs(order) do
                    local pick = pool[oi]
                    dbg(string.format("  ladder cand cell=%d mode=%s → Path:Find...",
                        pick.index, mode))
                    local path = Reachable(entity, pick.index)
                    if path then
                        dbg(string.format("    path OK len=%d [%s]",
                            #path, table.concat(path, ",")))
                        local result
                        if mode == "demolish" then
                            result = { index = pick.index, terrainId = "ladder",
                                       profId = nil, demolish = true }
                        else
                            result = { index = pick.index, terrainId = "ladder",
                                       profId = "builder", demolish = false }
                        end
                        dbg(string.format("→ PICK ladder cell=%d mode=%s", pick.index, mode))
                        return result
                    else
                        dbg(string.format("    path FAIL (ladder cell=%d mode=%s)",
                            pick.index, mode))
                    end
                end
                dbg("  ladder: все кандидаты недостижимы")
            end
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- 2. Обычные выделенные клетки
        -- ====================================================================
        local allowed = GetAllowed(kind)
        if not allowed then
            dbg("allowed=nil → NO PICK")
            return nil
        end

        local selected = GetSelectedCells()
        dbg(string.format("selected cells: %d", #selected))

        local candidates = {}
        for _, cell in ipairs(selected) do
            local fresh = IsCellFresh(cell)
            local allow = allowed[cell.terrainId] and true or false
            local reserved = Grid:IsReserved(cell.index, entity.id) and true or false

            if cell.terrainId ~= "ladder" and fresh and allow and not reserved then
                candidates[#candidates + 1] = cell
                dbg(string.format("  sel cell=%d terrain=%s fresh=%s allowed=%s reserved=%s → candidate",
                    cell.index, tostring(cell.terrainId), tostring(fresh),
                    tostring(allow), tostring(reserved)))
            else
                local reason = "?"
                if cell.terrainId == "ladder" then reason = "ladder (обрабатывается выше)"
                elseif not fresh then reason = "stale"
                elseif not allow then reason = "not allowed"
                elseif reserved then reason = "reserved" end
                dbg(string.format("  sel cell=%d terrain=%s fresh=%s allowed=%s reserved=%s → skip (%s)",
                    cell.index, tostring(cell.terrainId), tostring(fresh),
                    tostring(allow), tostring(reserved), reason))
            end
        end

        if #candidates == 0 then
            dbg("no candidates → NO PICK")
            return nil
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- 3. best (с профессией) / rest (без)
        -- ====================================================================
        local best, rest = {}, {}
        for _, cell in ipairs(candidates) do
            local prof = FindProfForTerrain(entity, cell.terrainId)
            if prof then
                best[#best + 1] = { index = cell.index, terrainId = cell.terrainId, profId = prof.id }
                dbg(string.format("  sel cell=%d terrain=%s prof=%s → best",
                    cell.index, tostring(cell.terrainId), tostring(prof.id)))
            else
                rest[#rest + 1] = { index = cell.index, terrainId = cell.terrainId }
                dbg(string.format("  sel cell=%d terrain=%s prof=nil → rest",
                    cell.index, tostring(cell.terrainId)))
            end
        end

        local pool = (#best > 0) and best or rest
        dbg(string.format("pool: best=%d rest=%d → используем %s",
            #best, #rest, (#best > 0) and "best" or "rest"))

        if #pool == 0 then
            dbg("empty pool → NO PICK")
            return nil
        end

        -- ====================================================================
        -- 4. Проверка достижимости
        -- ====================================================================
        local order = {}
        for i = 1, #pool do order[i] = i end
        for i = #order, 2, -1 do
            local j = math.random(i)
            order[i], order[j] = order[j], order[i]
        end

        for _, oi in ipairs(order) do
            local pick = pool[oi]
            dbg(string.format("  pick candidate cell=%d terrain=%s profId=%s → Path:Find...",
                pick.index, tostring(pick.terrainId), tostring(pick.profId)))
            local path = Reachable(entity, pick.index)
            if path then
                dbg(string.format("    path OK len=%d [%s]",
                    #path, table.concat(path, ",")))
                dbg(string.format("→ PICK cell=%d terrainId=%s profId=%s demolish=false",
                    pick.index, tostring(pick.terrainId), tostring(pick.profId)))
                return pick
            else
                dbg(string.format("    path FAIL (cell=%d terrain=%s)",
                    pick.index, tostring(pick.terrainId)))
            end
        end

        dbg("→ NO PICK (все кандидаты недостижимы)")
        return nil
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Слэш-команда
    -- ========================================================================
    SLASH_NSFIND1 = "/ns_find"
    SlashCmdList["NSFIND"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then
            Find.DEBUG = true
            print("[FIND] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            Find.DEBUG = false
            print("[FIND] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "dump" then
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if not Entities then
                print("[FIND] dump: Entities не загружен")
                return
            end
            print("[FIND] dump: прогон Pick для всех существ (без побочных эффектов)")
            local saved = Find.DEBUG
            Find.DEBUG = true
            for _, e in ipairs(Entities:GetAll()) do
                if e.opts and not e.opts.sleeping then
                    local ai = e.opts and e.opts.ai
                    if not ai or ai.state == "idle" then
                        print(string.format("[FIND] --- %s ---", tostring(e.opts.name)))
                        Find:Pick(e)
                    else
                        print(string.format("[FIND] %s: state=%s → пропуск dump",
                            tostring(e.opts.name), tostring(ai and ai.state)))
                    end
                end
            end
            Find.DEBUG = saved
        else
            print("[FIND] /ns_find on | off | dump")
        end
    end

    print("|cff00ffff[FIND]|r Find загружен (DEBUG=" .. tostring(Find.DEBUG) .. ")")

end)

-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local AI = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAI = AI

    -- Интервал тика (секунды).
    AI.INTERVAL = 1.0

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Один тик для одного существа.
    -- Пока — заглушка. Наполним, когда появятся State/Find/Path/Move/Work.
    -- ========================================================================
    function AI:TickEntity(entity)
        -- TODO: idle → поиск цели → moving → working
        return false
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Move.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Move
-- Движение от штатной точки к штатной точке.
--
-- ШТАТНЫЕ ТОЧКИ (единое правило, GetAnchorPos):
--   свёрнутая  (expanded=false) — TOPLEFT + ins + size/2   (верхний левый угол)
--   развёрнутая (expanded=true) — центр клетки
--
-- ЕДИНЫЙ ИСТОЧНИК ИСТИНЫ:
--   entity.posX / entity.posY — позиция в координатах поля.
--   entity.opts.expanded      — флаг размера.
--   Текстура (temp/anchor) — чистая функция от них (см. Entities/UI/Api).
--
-- СМЕНА expanded В ПУТИ:
--   Пересчитываем stepStart/stepEnd по новому expanded и корректируем
--   stepStartedAt так, чтобы posX/posY в момент смены не изменились.
--
-- ЗАВЕРШЕНИЕ ШАГА (t >= 1):
--   ПОРЯДОК ВАЖЕН: сначала обновляем данные (entity.index, posX/posY,
--   ResetStep, Grid:SetEntityName), ПОТОМ один раз перерисовываем.
--   Раньше StopMoving(to) звался ДО SetEntityName(to) — Api:Render для `to`
--   не находил существа и рисовал пустую клетку → иконка «пропадала на
--   секунду» до следующего тика. Теперь — нет.
--
-- stepTime = 100 − 99 × (vitality / 100)  секунд на клетку.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Move = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIMove = Move
    Move.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if Move.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[MOVE]|r", ...) end
    end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Move: Grid не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local QuestsField = NSQC4.QuestsField

    function Move:StepTime(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return 100 end
        local vit = tonumber(entity.opts.vitality) or 0
        local pct = vit / 100
        if pct < 0 then pct = 0 elseif pct > 1 then pct = 1 end
        return 100 - 99 * pct
    end

    local function CanStepInto(nextIndex, entity)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(nextIndex)
        if not c then return false end
        if c.terrain == "void" then return false end
        if c.terrain == "water" then
            local Resolve = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsResolve
            local canSwim = Resolve and Resolve.CanSwim and Resolve:CanSwim(entity) or false
            if not canSwim then return false end
        end
        if c.entityName then
            local me = entity.opts and entity.opts.name
            if not me or c.entityName ~= me then return false end
        end
        return true
    end

    function Move:ResetStep(entity)
        if not entity then return end
        entity.nextIndex     = nil
        entity.stepStartX    = nil; entity.stepStartY = nil
        entity.stepEndX      = nil; entity.stepEndY   = nil
        entity.stepStartedAt = nil; entity.stepDuration = nil
        entity.stepExpanded  = nil
    end

    local function IsExpanded(entity)
        return entity and entity.opts and entity.opts.expanded and true or false
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Зафиксировать начало шага: точки старта/финиша по ТЕКУЩЕМУ expanded.
    -- ========================================================================
    local function BeginStep(entity, nextIndex, expanded)
        local sX, sY = QuestsField:GetAnchorPos(entity.index, expanded)
        local eX, eY = QuestsField:GetAnchorPos(nextIndex, expanded)
        if not sX or not eX then return false end

        local EntitiesApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntitiesUIApi
        if EntitiesApi and EntitiesApi.HideAnchorAt then
            EntitiesApi:HideAnchorAt(entity.index)
        end

        entity.nextIndex     = nextIndex
        entity.stepStartX    = sX
        entity.stepStartY    = sY
        entity.stepEndX      = eX
        entity.stepEndY      = eY
        entity.stepStartedAt = GetTime()
        entity.stepDuration  = Move:StepTime(entity)
        entity.stepExpanded  = expanded
        entity.posX          = sX
        entity.posY          = sY
        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Пересчитать штатные точки шага под НОВЫЙ expanded, сохранив
    -- текущую позицию posX/posY и долю прохождения t.
    -- ========================================================================
    local function RebaseStepForExpanded(entity, expanded)
        if not entity.nextIndex then return end
        if not entity.stepStartX then return end

        local Px, Py = entity.posX, entity.posY
        if not Px or not Py then return end

        local sX, sY = QuestsField:GetAnchorPos(entity.index, expanded)
        local eX, eY = QuestsField:GetAnchorPos(entity.nextIndex, expanded)
        if not sX or not eX then return end

        local dx, dy = (eX - sX), (eY - sY)
        local len2 = dx * dx + dy * dy

        local tNew
        if len2 <= 0.0001 then
            tNew = 0
            entity.posX = sX
            entity.posY = sY
        else
            local tRaw = ((Px - sX) * dx + (Py - sY) * dy) / len2
            if tRaw < 0 then tRaw = 0 elseif tRaw > 1 then tRaw = 1 end
            tNew = tRaw
        end

        local now = GetTime()
        local dur = entity.stepDuration or 1
        if dur <= 0 then dur = 1 end

        entity.stepStartX    = sX
        entity.stepStartY    = sY
        entity.stepEndX      = eX
        entity.stepEndY      = eY
        entity.stepStartedAt = now - tNew * dur

        entity.posX = sX + (eX - sX) * tNew
        entity.posY = sY + (eY - sY) * tNew

        dbg(string.format("rebase expanded=%s t=%.3f pos=(%.1f,%.1f)",
            tostring(expanded), tNew, entity.posX, entity.posY))
    end

    function Move:SyncExpanded(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.nextIndex then return false end
        RebaseStepForExpanded(entity, IsExpanded(entity))
        return true
    end

    function Move:Advance(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return nil end
        local ai = entity.opts.ai
        if not ai or not ai.path or not ai.pathIdx then return nil end
        if not ai.target then
            ai.path = nil; ai.pathIdx = nil
            return nil
        end

        local path = ai.path
        local name = entity.opts.name or entity.id
        local now  = GetTime()
        local EntitiesApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntitiesUIApi

        -- --------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Старт нового шага
        -- --------------------------------------------------------------
        if not entity.nextIndex then
            if ai.pathIdx >= #path then return "arrived" end
            if ai.nextStepAt and now < ai.nextStepAt then return "wait" end

            local nxt = path[ai.pathIdx + 1]
            if not nxt then return nil end
            if not CanStepInto(nxt, entity) then
                ai.path = nil; ai.pathIdx = nil
                return nil
            end

            local expanded = IsExpanded(entity)
            if not BeginStep(entity, nxt, expanded) then return nil end

            dbg(string.format("%s: start %s → %s exp=%s dur=%.2f",
                tostring(name), tostring(entity.index), tostring(nxt),
                tostring(expanded), entity.stepDuration))
        end

        -- --------------------------------------------------------------
        -- СМЕНА expanded В ПУТИ: если флаг разошёлся с геометрией шага —
        -- пересчитать концы и t без рывка.
        -- --------------------------------------------------------------
        local curExpanded = IsExpanded(entity)
        if entity.stepExpanded ~= curExpanded then
            RebaseStepForExpanded(entity, curExpanded)
            entity.stepExpanded = curExpanded
        end

        local t = (now - entity.stepStartedAt) / entity.stepDuration
        if t >= 1 then t = 1 end

        local x = entity.stepStartX + (entity.stepEndX - entity.stepStartX) * t
        local y = entity.stepStartY + (entity.stepEndY - entity.stepStartY) * t
        entity.posX = x
        entity.posY = y

        if EntitiesApi and EntitiesApi.MoveAnchor then
            EntitiesApi:MoveAnchor(entity, x, y)
        end

        -- --------------------------------------------------------------
        -- ЗАВЕРШЕНИЕ ШАГА
        -- Порядок: СНАЧАЛА данные, ПОТОМ рендер.
        -- --------------------------------------------------------------
        if t >= 1 then
            local from = entity.index
            local to   = entity.nextIndex

            -- 1. Данные: позиция, сброс шага, имена в Grid.
            entity.index = to
            entity.posX  = entity.stepEndX
            entity.posY  = entity.stepEndY
            self:ResetStep(entity)   -- nextIndex=nil, step*=nil, stepExpanded=nil

            Grid:SetEntityName(from, nil)
            Grid:SetEntityName(to, entity.opts and entity.opts.name)

            ai.pathIdx    = ai.pathIdx + 1
            ai.nextStepAt = nil

            -- 2. Рендер: StopMoving видит согласованное состояние
            --    (entity.index=to, nextIndex=nil, Grid:GetEntity(to)=entity)
            --    и рисует anchor в `to`, а не пустую клетку.
            if EntitiesApi and EntitiesApi.StopMoving then
                EntitiesApi:StopMoving(entity, to)
            end
            -- `from` гарантированно очистить (на случай, если там остался
            -- temp/anchor от предыдущего шага).
            if EntitiesApi and EntitiesApi.RenderCellFromStorage then
                EntitiesApi:RenderCellFromStorage(from)
            end

            local Stats = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIStats
            if Stats and Stats.TryUpVitality then Stats:TryUpVitality(entity) end

            if ai.pathIdx >= #path then return "arrived" end
            return "moved"
        end

        return "moving"
    end

    SLASH_NSMOVE1 = "/ns_move"
    SlashCmdList["NSMOVE"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then Move.DEBUG = true; print("[MOVE] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then Move.DEBUG = false; print("[MOVE] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "dump" then
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if not Entities then return end
            for _, e in ipairs(Entities:GetAll()) do
                local ai = e.opts and e.opts.ai
                if ai then
                    print(string.format("[MOVE] %s idx=%s next=%s pos=(%.1f,%.1f) exp=%s state=%s",
                        tostring(e.opts.name), tostring(e.index), tostring(e.nextIndex),
                        e.posX or -1, e.posY or -1, tostring(e.opts.expanded), tostring(ai.state)))
                end
            end
        else print("[MOVE] /ns_move on | off | dump") end
    end

    print("|cff00ffff[MOVE]|r Move загружен (штатные точки, rebase при смене expanded, без пропадания на финише)")
end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Path.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Path
-- Поиск кратчайшего маршрута по сетке 10×10 (BFS, 4-связность).
--
-- ВАЖНО (модель движения):
--   Path:Find(startIndex, targetIndex, entity) считает от entity.index —
--   текущей клетки, где существо ЧИСЛИТСЯ. Пока существо идёт между
--   центрами клеток, entity.index не меняется (см. Move:Advance).
--   Значит, Path всегда строит маршрут от реальной текущей клетки.
--
-- Проходимость:
--   - старт: всегда проходим (существо там стоит)
--   - цель:  не void, не вода (если не canSwim)
--   - промежуточная: не void, не вода (если не canSwim), не занята другим
--
-- ОТЛАДКА:
--   /ns_path on | off | dump
--   Path.DEBUG = true/false
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Path = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIPath = Path
    Path.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if Path.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[PATH]|r", ...) end
    end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Path: Grid не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not CONST then return end
    local TOTAL = CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Восстановить путь по prev[].
    -- ========================================================================
    local function Reconstruct(prev, startIdx, targetIdx)
        local path = {}
        local cur = targetIdx
        while cur ~= nil do
            path[#path + 1] = cur
            if cur == startIdx then break end
            cur = prev[cur]
        end

        local n = #path
        for i = 1, math.floor(n / 2) do
            path[i], path[n - i + 1] = path[n - i + 1], path[i]
        end
        return path
    end

    local function CellInfo(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c then return string.format("cell=%s (нет клетки)", tostring(index)) end
        return string.format("cell=%s terrain=%s entityName=%s passable(raw)=%s",
            tostring(index), tostring(c.terrain), tostring(c.entityName),
            Grid:IsPassable(index) and "yes" or "no")
    end

    local function CellInfoEnt(index, entity)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c then return string.format("cell=%s (нет клетки)", tostring(index)) end
        return string.format("cell=%s terrain=%s entityName=%s passable(ent)=%s",
            tostring(index), tostring(c.terrain), tostring(c.entityName),
            Grid:IsPassable(index, entity) and "yes" or "no")
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- CanEnter — можно ли войти в клетку.
    --   startIndex — старт (всегда проходим)
    --   targetIndex — цель (не void, не вода без canSwim)
    --   иначе — промежуточная
    -- ========================================================================
    local function CanEnter(neighbor, targetIndex, startIndex, entity, canSwim)
        if neighbor == startIndex then
            return true, nil
        end

        if neighbor == targetIndex then
            local c = Grid:GetCell(neighbor)
            if not c then return false, "nocell" end
            if c.terrain == "void" then return false, "void" end
            if c.terrain == "water" and not canSwim then return false, "water" end
            return true, nil
        end

        local c = Grid:GetCell(neighbor)
        if not c then return false, "nocell" end
        if c.terrain == "void" then return false, "void" end
        if c.terrain == "water" and not canSwim then return false, "water" end

        if c.entityName then
            local myName = entity and entity.opts and entity.opts.name
            if not myName or c.entityName ~= myName then
                return false, "occupied:" .. tostring(c.entityName)
            end
            return true, nil
        end

        return true, nil
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Find — BFS.
    -- ========================================================================
    function Path:Find(startIndex, targetIndex, entity)
        startIndex  = tonumber(startIndex)  or 0
        targetIndex = tonumber(targetIndex) or 0

        if startIndex < 1 or startIndex > TOTAL then
            dbg(string.format("Find: start=%s вне диапазона", tostring(startIndex)))
            return nil
        end
        if targetIndex < 1 or targetIndex > TOTAL then
            dbg(string.format("Find: target=%s вне диапазона", tostring(targetIndex)))
            return nil
        end

        local canSwim = false
        local Resolve = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsResolve
        if entity and Resolve and Resolve.CanSwim then
            canSwim = Resolve:CanSwim(entity) and true or false
        end

        local eName = entity and entity.opts and entity.opts.name or "?"

        dbg("=========================================================")
        dbg(string.format("Find start=%s target=%s entity=%s canSwim=%s",
            tostring(startIndex), tostring(targetIndex), tostring(eName), tostring(canSwim)))
        dbg("  " .. CellInfo(startIndex))
        dbg("  " .. CellInfoEnt(targetIndex, entity))

        if startIndex == targetIndex then
            dbg("  start == target → путь {start}")
            return { startIndex }
        end

        local prev    = {}
        local visited = {}
        local queue   = { startIndex }
        local head    = 1

        visited[startIndex] = true
        prev[startIndex]    = nil

        local blockVoid, blockWater, blockOccupied, blockNoCell = {}, {}, {}, {}
        local visitedCount = 0

        local function noteBlock(reason, neighbor)
            if reason == "void" then blockVoid[#blockVoid + 1] = neighbor
            elseif reason == "water" then blockWater[#blockWater + 1] = neighbor
            elseif reason == "nocell" then blockNoCell[#blockNoCell + 1] = neighbor
            elseif reason and reason:sub(1, 8) == "occupied" then
                blockOccupied[#blockOccupied + 1] = neighbor
            end
        end

        while head <= #queue do
            local cur = queue[head]
            head = head + 1
            visitedCount = visitedCount + 1

            local neighbors = Grid:Neighbors(cur)
            if Path.DEBUG then
                local c = Grid:GetCell(cur)
                dbg(string.format("BFS head=%d cur=%d terrain=%s entityName=%s neighbors={%s}",
                    head - 1, cur, tostring(c and c.terrain), tostring(c and c.entityName),
                    table.concat(neighbors, ",")))
            end

            for _, neighbor in ipairs(neighbors) do
                if not visited[neighbor] then
                    local ok, reason = CanEnter(neighbor, targetIndex, startIndex, entity, canSwim)
                    if ok then
                        visited[neighbor] = true
                        prev[neighbor]    = cur
                        queue[#queue + 1] = neighbor

                        if Path.DEBUG then
                            local c = Grid:GetCell(neighbor)
                            dbg(string.format("    neighbor=%d terrain=%s entityName=%s → enqueue",
                                neighbor, tostring(c and c.terrain), tostring(c and c.entityName)))
                        end

                        if neighbor == targetIndex then
                            local path = Reconstruct(prev, startIndex, targetIndex)
                            dbg(string.format("BFS done: visited=%d", visitedCount))
                            dbg(string.format("path found: len=%d [%s]",
                                #path, table.concat(path, ",")))
                            dbg("=========================================================")
                            return path
                        end
                    else
                        noteBlock(reason, neighbor)
                        if Path.DEBUG then
                            local c = Grid:GetCell(neighbor)
                            dbg(string.format("    neighbor=%d terrain=%s entityName=%s → block (%s)",
                                neighbor, tostring(c and c.terrain),
                                tostring(c and c.entityName), tostring(reason)))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if Path.DEBUG then
            dbg(string.format("BFS done: visited=%d queue exhausted", visitedCount))
            dbg("path NOT found")
            dbg(string.format("block summary: void=%d water=%d occupied=%d nocell=%d",
                #blockVoid, #blockWater, #blockOccupied, #blockNoCell))
            local function fmtList(t)
                if #t == 0 then return "[]" end
                return "[" .. table.concat(t, ",") .. "]"
            end
            if #blockVoid > 0     then dbg("  void:     " .. fmtList(blockVoid))     end
            if #blockWater > 0    then dbg("  water:    " .. fmtList(blockWater))    end
            if #blockOccupied > 0 then dbg("  occupied: " .. fmtList(blockOccupied)) end
            if #blockNoCell > 0   then dbg("  nocell:   " .. fmtList(blockNoCell))   end
            dbg("=========================================================")
        end

        return nil
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- /ns_path
    -- ========================================================================
    SLASH_NSPATH1 = "/ns_path"
    SlashCmdList["NSPATH"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then
            Path.DEBUG = true
            print("[PATH] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            Path.DEBUG = false
            print("[PATH] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "dump" then
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if not Entities then return end
            for _, e in ipairs(Entities:GetAll()) do
                local ai = e.opts and e.opts.ai
                if ai and ai.target then
                    local path = Path:Find(e.index, ai.target, e)
                    if path then
                        print(string.format("  %s idx=%s target=%s → len=%d [%s]",
                            tostring(e.opts.name), tostring(e.index), tostring(ai.target),
                            #path, table.concat(path, ",")))
                    else
                        print(string.format("  %s idx=%s target=%s → NO PATH",
                            tostring(e.opts.name), tostring(e.index), tostring(ai.target)))
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            print("[PATH] /ns_path on | off | dump")
        end
    end

    print("|cff00ffff[PATH]|r Path загружен (DEBUG=" .. tostring(Path.DEBUG) .. ")")
end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Sound.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Sound
