-- Короткие звуки событий: работа по terrain, старт к цели по kind.
-- Без стопов — короткие, накладываются.
--
-- Sound:PlayTerrain(terrainId)  — звук работы по типу ландшафта.
-- Sound:PlayKind(kind)          — "goblin" → gob.ogg, "dwarf" → gom.ogg
--
-- ladder → временно stone (файла lad.ogg нет).
-- pool   → временно water/stone (файла нет).
-- water  → нет файла, звук не играет.
--
-- Отладка: /run NSQC4.QuestsDfAISound.DEBUG = true
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Sound = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAISound = Sound

    Sound.DEBUG   = false
    Sound.ENABLED = true

    local IMG_PATH = "Interface\\AddOns\\NSQC4\\Media\\Sounds\\"

    -- terrainId → файл. ladder/pool — временные мапы (файлов нет).
    local TERRAIN_SOUND = {
        earth  = "0uz.ogg",
        grass  = "00f.ogg",
        stone  = "0ka.ogg",
        tree   = "00t.ogg",
        ladder = "0ka.ogg",   -- временно: звук камня (стройка/лом)
        pool   = "0uz.ogg",   -- временно: звук земли
        -- water — файла нет, не играем
    }

    local KIND_SOUND = {
        goblin = "gob.ogg",
        dwarf  = "gom.ogg",
    }

    local function dbg(...)
        if Sound.DEBUG then print("|cffffaa00[SND]|r", ...) end
    end

    function Sound:PlayFile(file)
        if not Sound.ENABLED then return end
        if not file or file == "" then return end
        local path = IMG_PATH .. file
        local willPlay, handle = PlaySoundFile(path, "SFX")
        dbg("play", file, willPlay and "ok" or "fail", tostring(handle))
    end

    function Sound:PlayTerrain(terrainId)
        local file = TERRAIN_SOUND[terrainId]
        if not file then
            dbg("no terrain sound for", tostring(terrainId))
            return
        end
        self:PlayFile(file)
    end

    function Sound:PlayKind(kind)
        local file = KIND_SOUND[kind]
        if not file then
            dbg("no kind sound for", tostring(kind))
            return
        end
        self:PlayFile(file)
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/State.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / State
-- Единственное место, где живёт entity.opts.ai.
--
-- ФИКСЫ:
--   1. ReserveBuildingViaGrid / ReleaseBuildingViaGrid проверяют
--      Grid:IsBuilding(idx). Для не-построек — skip. Иначе SetMoving для
--      tree/earth/grass всегда откатывался в idle.
--   2. SetIdle / Clear сбрасывают entity.nextIndex / posX / posY / step*
--      и зовут UIApi:StopMoving — иначе при сбросе в idle посреди шага
--      остаётся temp-иконка и «висящая» позиция; следующий SetMoving
--      стартует из неверной точки.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local State = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIState = State
    State.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if State.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[STATE]|r", ...) end
    end

    local function GetGrid() return NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Сброс полей шага + temp-иконки (MoveAnchor).
    -- ========================================================================
    local function ResetStepFields(entity, renderIndex)
        if not entity then return end
        entity.nextIndex     = nil
        entity.posX          = nil
        entity.posY          = nil
        entity.stepStartX    = nil
        entity.stepStartY    = nil
        entity.stepEndX      = nil
        entity.stepEndY      = nil
        entity.stepStartedAt = nil
        entity.stepDuration  = nil

        local EntitiesApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntitiesUIApi
        if EntitiesApi and EntitiesApi.StopMoving then
            EntitiesApi:StopMoving(entity, renderIndex or entity.index)
        end
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Резерв постройки (только если это реально постройка).
    -- ========================================================================
    local function ReserveBuildingViaGrid(idx, entity)
        if not idx or not entity then return true end
        local Grid = GetGrid()
        if not Grid then return true end

        if not (Grid.IsBuilding and Grid:IsBuilding(idx)) then
            dbg(string.format("  IsBuilding(%s)=false → ReserveBuilding skip (не постройка)",
                tostring(idx)))
            return true
        end

        if not Grid.ReserveBuilding then
            dbg(string.format("  ReserveBuilding недоступен → skip"))
            return true
        end

        local ok = Grid:ReserveBuilding(idx, entity)
        if ok then
            dbg(string.format("  ReserveBuilding(%s) → ok (%s)",
                tostring(idx), tostring(entity.opts and entity.opts.name)))
        else
            dbg(string.format("  ReserveBuilding(%s) → ЗАНЯТА (%s)",
                tostring(idx), tostring(entity.opts and entity.opts.name)))
        end
        return ok
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Снять резерв постройки (только если это реально постройка).
    -- ========================================================================
    local function ReleaseBuildingViaGrid(idx, entityId)
        if not idx or not entityId then return end
        local Grid = GetGrid()
        if not Grid then return end

        if not (Grid.IsBuilding and Grid:IsBuilding(idx)) then
            dbg(string.format("  IsBuilding(%s)=false → ReleaseBuilding skip", tostring(idx)))
            return
        end

        if not Grid.ReleaseBuilding then return end

        if Grid:ReleaseBuilding(idx, entityId) then
            dbg(string.format("  ReleaseBuilding(%s) → ok (%s)", tostring(idx), tostring(entityId)))
        else
            dbg(string.format("  ReleaseBuilding(%s) → не наш (%s)", tostring(idx), tostring(entityId)))
        end
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Get / состояние
    -- ========================================================================
    function State:Get(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return nil end
        local ai = entity.opts.ai
        if not ai then
            ai = { state = "idle" }
            entity.opts.ai = ai
            dbg(string.format("Get: создан ai для %s",
                tostring(entity.opts.name or entity.id)))
        end
        if not ai.state then ai.state = "idle" end
        return ai
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- carrying
    -- ========================================================================
    function State:SetCarrying(entity, material, count)
        local ai = self:Get(entity)
        if not ai then return false end
        ai.carrying = { material = material, count = count or 1 }
        dbg(string.format("SetCarrying %s material=%s count=%s",
            tostring(entity.opts and entity.opts.name),
            tostring(material), tostring(count or 1)))
        return true
    end

    function State:ClearCarrying(entity)
        local ai = self:Get(entity)
        if ai and ai.carrying then
            dbg(string.format("ClearCarrying %s (was %s x%s)",
                tostring(entity.opts and entity.opts.name),
                tostring(ai.carrying.material), tostring(ai.carrying.count)))
        end
        if ai then ai.carrying = nil end
    end

    function State:GetCarrying(entity)
        local ai = self:Get(entity)
        return ai and ai.carrying or nil
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Clear / SetIdle
    -- ========================================================================
    function State:Clear(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return end
        local ai = entity.opts.ai
        if not ai then return end

        dbg(string.format("Clear %s old.state=%s old.target=%s",
            tostring(entity.opts.name or entity.id),
            tostring(ai.state), tostring(ai.target)))

        local Grid = GetGrid()
        if ai.target and Grid and Grid.Release then
            Grid:Release(ai.target, entity.id)
            dbg(string.format("  Release(%s) → ok", tostring(ai.target)))
        end

        local bIdx = ai.workTarget or ai.target
        ReleaseBuildingViaGrid(bIdx, entity.id)

        -- ФИКС: сброс полей шага + temp
        ResetStepFields(entity, entity.index)

        entity.opts.ai = nil
    end

    function State:SetIdle(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end
        local old = entity.opts.ai

        dbg("=========================================================")
        dbg(string.format("SetIdle %s old.state=%s old.target=%s",
            tostring(entity.opts.name or entity.id),
            tostring(old and old.state), tostring(old and old.target)))

        if old then
            local Grid = GetGrid()
            if old.target and Grid and Grid.Release then
                Grid:Release(old.target, entity.id)
                dbg(string.format("  Release(%s) → ok", tostring(old.target)))
            end
            local bIdx = old.workTarget or old.target
            ReleaseBuildingViaGrid(bIdx, entity.id)
        end

        -- ФИКС: сброс полей шага + temp, чтобы следующий SetMoving
        -- стартовал из центра текущей клетки, а не из «висящей» точки.
        ResetStepFields(entity, entity.index)

        entity.opts.ai = { state = "idle" }
        dbg("→ state=idle")
        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- SetMoving: резерв — только для построек.
    -- ========================================================================
    function State:SetMoving(entity, target, terrainId, profId, path, opts)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end
        if not target or not path or #path == 0 then
            dbg(string.format("SetMoving SKIP: target=%s path=%s len=%s",
                tostring(target), tostring(path ~= nil), tostring(path and #path)))
            return false
        end

        opts = opts or {}
        local wt = opts.workTarget or target

        dbg("=========================================================")
        dbg(string.format("SetMoving %s → target=%s wt=%s terrain=%s prof=%s material=%s",
            tostring(entity.opts.name or entity.id),
            tostring(target), tostring(wt), tostring(terrainId), tostring(profId),
            tostring(opts.material)))
        dbg(string.format("  path len=%d [%s]", #path, table.concat(path, ",")))

        -- снять старый резерв
        local old = entity.opts.ai
        if old then
            local Grid = GetGrid()
            if old.target and old.target ~= target and Grid and Grid.Release then
                Grid:Release(old.target, entity.id)
                dbg(string.format("  Release old.target=%s → ok", tostring(old.target)))
            end
            local oldWork = old.workTarget or old.target
            if oldWork and oldWork ~= wt then
                ReleaseBuildingViaGrid(oldWork, entity.id)
            end
        end

        -- резерв клетки
        local Grid = GetGrid()
        if Grid and Grid.Reserve then
            Grid:Reserve(target, entity.id)
            dbg(string.format("  Reserve(target=%s) → ok", tostring(target)))
        end

        -- резерв постройки (только если wt — реальная постройка)
        if wt then
            local ok = ReserveBuildingViaGrid(wt, entity)
            if not ok then
                if Grid and Grid.Release then
                    Grid:Release(target, entity.id)
                    dbg(string.format("  Release(target=%s) → откат", tostring(target)))
                end
                entity.opts.ai = { state = "idle" }
                dbg(string.format("→ SetMoving FAIL: building %s занята — откат в idle",
                    tostring(wt)))
                return false
            end
        end

        entity.opts.ai = {
            state      = "moving",
            target     = target,
            workTarget = wt,
            terrainId  = terrainId,
            profId     = profId,
            path       = path,
            pathIdx    = 1,
            nextStepAt = GetTime(),
            work       = nil,
            buildWork  = nil,
            carrying   = old and old.carrying or nil,
        }

        -- ФИКС: сброс «висящих» полей шага от предыдущего движения.
        -- Не трогаем posX/posY — Move:Advance их сам пересчитает через
        -- EnsurePos (если nil → центр текущей клетки).
        entity.nextIndex     = nil
        entity.stepStartX    = nil
        entity.stepStartY    = nil
        entity.stepEndX      = nil
        entity.stepEndY      = nil
        entity.stepStartedAt = nil
        entity.stepDuration  = nil

        dbg(string.format("→ state=moving OK (target=%s wt=%s)",
            tostring(target), tostring(wt)))
        return true
    end

    function State:SetWorking(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end
        local ai = self:Get(entity)
        if not ai.target then
            dbg(string.format("SetWorking SKIP: %s — нет target",
                tostring(entity.opts.name or entity.id)))
            return false
        end
        ai.state = "working"
        ai.path = nil
        ai.pathIdx = nil
        ai.nextStepAt = nil
        ai.work = ai.work or {}

        -- сброс полей шага (существо уже в центре целевой клетки)
        entity.nextIndex     = nil
        entity.stepStartX    = nil
        entity.stepStartY    = nil
        entity.stepEndX      = nil
        entity.stepEndY      = nil
        entity.stepStartedAt = nil
        entity.stepDuration  = nil

        dbg(string.format("SetWorking %s at %s",
            tostring(entity.opts.name or entity.id), tostring(ai.target)))
        return true
    end

    function State:IsIdle(entity)
        local ai = self:Get(entity)
        return ai and ai.state == "idle"
    end
    function State:IsMoving(entity)
        local ai = self:Get(entity)
        return ai and ai.state == "moving"
    end
    function State:IsWorking(entity)
        local ai = self:Get(entity)
        return ai and ai.state == "working"
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Слэш-команда
    -- ========================================================================
    SLASH_NSSTATE1 = "/ns_state"
    SlashCmdList["NSSTATE"] = function(msg)
        local cmd, arg = (msg or ""):match("^(%S*)%s*(.-)$")
        cmd = (cmd or ""):lower()
        arg = arg or ""

        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then
            State.DEBUG = true
            print("[STATE] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            State.DEBUG = false
            print("[STATE] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "dump" then
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if not Entities then
                print("[STATE] dump: Entities не загружен")
                return
            end
            local list = Entities:GetAll()
            print(string.format("[STATE] dump: существ %d", #list))
            for _, e in ipairs(list) do
                local ai = e.opts and e.opts.ai
                if ai then
                    local carrying = ai.carrying
                        and string.format("%s×%d", tostring(ai.carrying.material),
                                                    tonumber(ai.carrying.count) or 1)
                        or "-"
                    print(string.format("  %s idx=%s next=%s state=%s target=%s wt=%s terrain=%s prof=%s pathLen=%s carrying=%s",
                        tostring(e.opts.name or e.id),
                        tostring(e.index),
                        tostring(e.nextIndex),
                        tostring(ai.state),
                        tostring(ai.target),
                        tostring(ai.workTarget),
                        tostring(ai.terrainId),
                        tostring(ai.profId),
                        tostring(ai.path and #ai.path),
                        carrying))
                end
            end
        elseif cmd == "clear" then
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if not Entities then return end
            if arg ~= "" then
                local e = Entities:GetByName(arg)
                if e then
                    State:SetIdle(e)
                    print("[STATE] clear " .. arg)
                else
                    print("[STATE] не найдено: " .. arg)
                end
            else
                for _, e in ipairs(Entities:GetAll()) do
                    if e.opts then State:SetIdle(e) end
                end
                print("[STATE] clear all")
            end
        else
            print("[STATE] /ns_state on | off | dump | clear [name]")
        end
    end

    print("|cff00ffff[STATE]|r State загружен (DEBUG=" .. tostring(State.DEBUG) .. ")")

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Stats.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Stats
-- Прокачка характеристик существ + отладка.
--
-- TryUpVitality — живучесть, шанс int/10000, джекпот (roll == 1) на max_*.
-- TryUpStat(entity, statName) — сила/ловкость/интеллект, шанс int/10000,
--   без джекпота (джекпот уже даёт TryUpVitality на случайный max_*).
--
-- Отладка:
--   /ns_stats on | off | dump | min | reset
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Stats = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIStats = Stats

    Stats.DEBUG = false

    local MAX_CAP = 100

    local CAP_KEYS = {
        { key = "max_skills_max",  label = "skills_max"  },
        { key = "max_strength",    label = "strength"    },
        { key = "max_agility",     label = "agility"     },
        { key = "max_vitality",    label = "vitality"    },
        { key = "max_intellect",   label = "intellect"   },
    }

    -- Какой стат к какому max_*
    local STAT_MAX = {
        strength  = "max_strength",
        agility   = "max_agility",
        intellect = "max_intellect",
        vitality  = "max_vitality",
    }

    local STAT_LABEL = {
        strength  = "Сила",
        agility   = "Ловкость",
        intellect = "Интеллект",
        vitality  = "Живучесть",
    }

    local dbg = {
        calls        = 0,
        earlyExitInt = 0,
        rolls        = 0,
        successes    = 0,
        jacks        = 0,
        capsRaised   = 0,
        statRolls    = 0,
        statSuccess  = 0,
        sumChance    = 0,
        minRoll      = nil,
        minRollName  = nil,
        minRollInt   = nil,
        startedAt    = GetTime(),
    }
    Stats._dbg = dbg

    local function UpdateEntityMin(opts, roll, int)
        local now = GetTime()
        local em = opts._minRoll
        if not em then
            opts._minRoll = { roll = roll, int = int, firstAt = now, at = now }
            return true
        end
        if not em.firstAt then em.firstAt = now end
        if not em.at then em.at = now end
        if roll < em.roll then
            em.roll = roll
            em.int  = int
            em.at   = now
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    local function GetEntityMin(opts)
        return opts and opts._minRoll or nil
    end

    local function EntityElapsed(opts)
        local em = opts and opts._minRoll
        if not em or not em.firstAt then return 0 end
        return math.floor(GetTime() - em.firstAt)
    end

    local function RaiseRandomCap(opts)
        local pool = {}
        for _, row in ipairs(CAP_KEYS) do
            local v = tonumber(opts[row.key]) or 0
            if v < MAX_CAP then
                pool[#pool + 1] = row
            end
        end
        if #pool == 0 then return nil end

        local pick = pool[math.random(#pool)]
        local old = tonumber(opts[pick.key]) or 0
        opts[pick.key] = old + 1
        return pick.label, old, opts[pick.key]
    end

    local function RefreshGlobalMin()
        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return end
        local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
        local TOTAL = CONST and (CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS) or 100

        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            local e = Grid:GetEntity(i)
            if e and e.opts and e.opts._minRoll then
                local em = e.opts._minRoll
                if dbg.minRoll == nil or em.roll < dbg.minRoll then
                    dbg.minRoll     = em.roll
                    dbg.minRollName = tostring(e.opts.name or e.id)
                    dbg.minRollInt  = em.int
                end
            end
        end
    end
    Stats._RefreshGlobalMin = RefreshGlobalMin

    local function PrintSummary(tag)
        local elapsed = GetTime() - dbg.startedAt
        local avgChance = (dbg.rolls > 0) and (dbg.sumChance / dbg.rolls) or 0
        local expected  = avgChance * dbg.rolls
        local minStr = dbg.minRoll
            and string.format("%d(%s,int=%d)", dbg.minRoll,
                tostring(dbg.minRollName), dbg.minRollInt)
            or "nil"

        print(string.format(
            "[STATS %s] t=%ds calls=%d rolls=%d succ=%d caps=%d jack=%d statRolls=%d statSucc=%d chance=%.4f%% exp=%.3f min=%s earlyInt=%d",
            tag or "", math.floor(elapsed),
            dbg.calls, dbg.rolls, dbg.successes, dbg.capsRaised, dbg.jacks,
            dbg.statRolls, dbg.statSuccess,
            avgChance * 100, expected, minStr,
            dbg.earlyExitInt))
    end

    local function PrintPerEntity()
        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return end
        local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
        local TOTAL = CONST and (CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS) or 100

        local parts = {}
        local list = {}
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            local e = Grid:GetEntity(i)
            if e and e.opts and e.opts._minRoll then
                list[#list + 1] = {
                    name = tostring(e.opts.name or e.id),
                    min  = e.opts._minRoll.roll,
                    sec  = EntityElapsed(e.opts),
                }
            end
        end
        table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.min < b.min end)
        for _, r in ipairs(list) do
            parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("%s за %dс min=%d", r.name, r.sec, r.min)
        end

        if #parts == 0 then
            print("[STATS] сущ: —")
        else
            print("[STATS] сущ: " .. table.concat(parts, " | "))
        end
    end

    function Stats:ResetStats()
        dbg.calls        = 0
        dbg.earlyExitInt = 0
        dbg.rolls        = 0
        dbg.successes    = 0
        dbg.jacks        = 0
        dbg.capsRaised   = 0
        dbg.statRolls    = 0
        dbg.statSuccess  = 0
        dbg.sumChance    = 0
        dbg.minRoll      = nil
        dbg.minRollName  = nil
        dbg.minRollInt   = nil
        dbg.startedAt    = GetTime()

        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
        local TOTAL = CONST and (CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS) or 100
        if Grid then
            for i = 1, TOTAL do
                local e = Grid:GetEntity(i)
                if e and e.opts then e.opts._minRoll = nil end
            end
        end

        print("[STATS] счётчики и минимумы сброшены.")
    end

    function Stats:DumpStats()
        RefreshGlobalMin()
        PrintSummary("dump")
        PrintPerEntity()
    end

    function Stats:DumpMin()
        RefreshGlobalMin()
        PrintSummary("min")
        PrintPerEntity()
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Живучесть (с джекпотом на max_*)
    -- ========================================================================
    function Stats:TryUpVitality(entity)
        dbg.calls = dbg.calls + 1

        if not entity or not entity.opts then
            if Stats.DEBUG then print("[STATS] skip: no entity/opts") end
            return false
        end

        local opts = entity.opts
        local name = tostring(opts.name or entity.id)

        local int = tonumber(opts.intellect) or 0
        if int <= 0 then
            dbg.earlyExitInt = dbg.earlyExitInt + 1
            if Stats.DEBUG then
                print(string.format("[STATS] %s skip int=0", name))
            end
            return false
        end

        local chance = int / 10000
        if chance > 1 then chance = 1 end
        dbg.sumChance = dbg.sumChance + chance
        dbg.rolls     = dbg.rolls + 1

        local roll = math.random(1, 10000)
        local hit  = (roll <= int)

        local newEntityMin = UpdateEntityMin(opts, roll, int)

        local newGlobalMin = false
        if dbg.minRoll == nil or roll < dbg.minRoll then
            dbg.minRoll     = roll
            dbg.minRollName = name
            dbg.minRollInt  = int
            newGlobalMin    = true
        end

        local capLabel, capOld, capNew
        if roll == 1 then
            dbg.jacks = dbg.jacks + 1
            capLabel, capOld, capNew = RaiseRandomCap(opts)
            if capLabel then
                dbg.capsRaised = dbg.capsRaised + 1
            end
        end

        local vit    = tonumber(opts.vitality)     or 0
        local maxVit = tonumber(opts.max_vitality) or 0
        local vitUp  = false
        if hit and maxVit > 0 and vit < maxVit then
            opts.vitality = vit + 1
            dbg.successes = dbg.successes + 1
            vitUp = true
        end

        if Stats.DEBUG then
            local em = GetEntityMin(opts)
            local marks = ""
            if newGlobalMin then marks = marks .. " *G" end
            if newEntityMin then marks = marks .. " *E" end
            if roll == 1 then marks = marks .. " *JACK" end

            local capStr = ""
            if capLabel then
                capStr = string.format(" CAP+1 %s %d→%d", capLabel, capOld, capNew)
            elseif roll == 1 then
                capStr = " CAP+1 none (все 100)"
            end

            print(string.format(
                "[STATS] %s за %dс int=%d p=%.4f%% roll=%d/%d %s vit=%d/%d r=%d s=%d caps=%d min=%s/%s%s%s",
                name, EntityElapsed(opts),
                int, chance * 100, roll, 10000,
                hit and (vitUp and "HIT" or "HIT(novit)") or "miss",
                opts.vitality, maxVit,
                dbg.rolls, dbg.successes, dbg.capsRaised,
                em and tostring(em.roll) or "-",
                dbg.minRoll and tostring(dbg.minRoll) or "-",
                capStr, marks))
        end

        return vitUp or (capLabel ~= nil)
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Профильный стат (сила / ловкость / интеллект) — без джекпота.
    -- Шанс int/10000. +1 к текущему, если < max_<stat>.
    -- ========================================================================
    function Stats:TryUpStat(entity, statName)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end
        if not statName then return false end

        local opts = entity.opts
        local name = tostring(opts.name or entity.id)

        local maxKey = STAT_MAX[statName]
        if not maxKey then return false end

        local int = tonumber(opts.intellect) or 0
        if int <= 0 then return false end

        local cur = tonumber(opts[statName]) or 0
        local max = tonumber(opts[maxKey]) or 0
        if max <= 0 or cur >= max then return false end

        dbg.statRolls = dbg.statRolls + 1

        local roll = math.random(1, 10000)
        local hit  = (roll <= int)
        if not hit then return false end

        opts[statName] = cur + 1
        dbg.statSuccess = dbg.statSuccess + 1

        if Stats.DEBUG then
            print(string.format(
                "[STATS] %s %s +1: %d→%d (roll=%d/%d int=%d)",
                name, STAT_LABEL[statName] or statName,
                cur, opts[statName], roll, 10000, int))
        end
        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Слэш-команда
    -- ========================================================================
    SLASH_NSSTATS1 = "/ns_stats"
    SlashCmdList["NSSTATS"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "dump" then
            Stats:DumpStats()
        elseif cmd == "min" then
            Stats:DumpMin()
        elseif cmd == "on" then
            Stats.DEBUG = true
            print("[STATS] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            Stats.DEBUG = false
            print("[STATS] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "reset" then
            Stats:ResetStats()
        else
            print("[STATS] /ns_stats on | off | dump | min | reset")
        end
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Tick.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Tick
-- Диспетчер поведения существ.
--
-- Вызывается из Ticker:Tick() через AI:TickEntity(entity).
-- Смотрит kind + behavior, ищет Behavior[kind][behavior]:Tick.
-- Если нет — Default:Tick(entity).
--
-- Сам ничего не делает: ни поиска, ни пути, ни работы.
-- Только выбирает, чьё поведение выполнять.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local AI = NSQC4.QuestsDfAI
    if not AI then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Tick: AI/Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local Default = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIDefault
    if not Default then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Tick: AI/Default.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    -- Переопределяем заглушку из AI/Init.lua.
    function AI:TickEntity(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end

        local kind     = entity.kind or entity.opts.kind
        local behavior = entity.opts.behavior or "peaceful"

        -- Прямой поиск: Behavior[kind][behavior]:Tick
        local Registry = NSQC4.QuestsDfBehavior
        local byKind   = Registry and kind and Registry[kind]
        local handler  = byKind and byKind[behavior]

        if handler and handler.Tick then
            return handler.Tick(entity)
        end

        -- Fallback
        return Default:Tick(entity)
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Ticker.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Ticker
-- Один глобальный OnUpdate. Раз в INTERVAL секунд проходит по всем
-- клеткам Grid и для каждого существа зовёт AI:TickEntity(entity).
--
-- ПРИОРИТЕТ (два прохода):
--   1. withProf — существа, у которых есть «своя» профессия:
--        - под СТРОЙКУ (builder) — если есть недостроенные buildings;
--        - под выделенную клетку (Find:HasProfForSelected).
--      Они тикают ПЕРВЫМИ и резервируют «свои» клетки (buildings).
--   2. withoutProf — все остальные. Им достаётся то, что осталось.
--
-- ФИКС: frame:Show() в EnsureFrame — без Show OnUpdate не тикает.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local AI = NSQC4.QuestsDfAI
    if not AI then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Ticker: AI/Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not CONST then return end
    local TOTAL = CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r AI/Ticker: Grid не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local Ticker = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAITicker = Ticker

    local frame       = nil
    local accumulator = 0

    local function EnsureFrame()
        if frame then return frame end
        frame = CreateFrame("Frame")
        frame:SetScript("OnUpdate", function(_, elapsed)
            accumulator = accumulator + elapsed
            if accumulator < AI.INTERVAL then return end
            accumulator = accumulator - AI.INTERVAL
            Ticker:Tick()
        end)
        frame:Show()   -- ФИКС: без Show OnUpdate не тикает
        return frame
    end

    function Ticker:Tick()
        local Find = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIFind
        local withProf, withoutProf = {}, {}

        -- Есть ли недостроенные постройки?
        local hasBuildings = false
        if Grid.GetAllBuildings then
            local b = Grid:GetAllBuildings()
            for _, rec in pairs(b) do
                if rec and tonumber(rec.hp) and tonumber(rec.max) and rec.hp < rec.max then
                    hasBuildings = true
                    break
                end
            end
        end

        -- 1. Разделяем существ на две группы
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            local entity = Grid:GetEntity(i)
            if entity and entity.opts then
                local hasProf = false
                if Find and Find.HasProfForSelected then
                    hasProf = Find:HasProfForSelected(entity)
                end
                if hasProf then
                    withProf[#withProf + 1] = entity
                else
                    withoutProf[#withoutProf + 1] = entity
                end
            end
        end

        -- 2. Сначала тикают «профи» — они забирают свои клетки
        for _, entity in ipairs(withProf) do
            AI:TickEntity(entity)
        end

        -- 3. Потом все остальные — берут что осталось
        for _, entity in ipairs(withoutProf) do
            AI:TickEntity(entity)
        end
    end

    function Ticker:Start()
        EnsureFrame()
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/AI/Work.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / AI / Work
-- Стройка лестницы: ПОРЦИЯМИ ПО 1 МАТЕРИАЛУ.
--
-- ЛОГИКА TickBuild:
--   stored = Grid:GetStored(bIdx) — сколько материалов лежит в клетке лестницы.
--   hpPerMaterial = 100 (из TerrainData:GetLadderParams(material)).
--
--   1. Если stored <= 0:
--        return false, "need_material"
--   2. Иначе строим:
--        clicksTotal = CalcClicks("builder", "agility", totalHP, entity)
--        stepDamage  = totalHP / clicksTotal
--        за каждый клик:
--          hp += stepDamage
--          накопленный счётчик HP за этот материал += stepDamage
--          если >= hpPerMaterial:
--             Grid:TakeStored(bIdx, 1)
--             hpSinceMaterial = 0
--   3. DONE только когда hp >= max.
--
-- ЗВУК РАБОТЫ:
--   Убран из тика. Теперь звук — в Hover.lua (при наведении на работающее
--   существо, раз в SOUND_INTERVAL секунд). См. Field/UI/API/Hover.lua.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Work = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfAIWork = Work
    Work.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if Work.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[WORK]|r", ...) end
    end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then return end

    local TERRAIN_TO_STAT = {
        tree  = { stat = "agility",   vitality = true  },
        stone = { stat = "strength",  vitality = true  },
        grass = { stat = "intellect", vitality = false },
    }

    local function LadderParams(material)
        local Data = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainData
        if Data and Data.GetLadderParams then return Data:GetLadderParams(material) end
        return 100, 100
    end

    local function CalcClicks(profId, stat, hardness, entity)
        hardness = tonumber(hardness) or 0
        if hardness <= 0 then return 1 end
        local ProfApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsApi
        local Resolve = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsResolve
        local profDef = profId and ProfApi and ProfApi:Get(profId) or nil
        if profDef and profDef.clicks and Resolve then
            local pp = Resolve:ProfPct(entity, profDef.terrain)
            local sp = Resolve:StatPct(entity, stat or profDef.stat)
            local total = profDef.clicks(hardness, pp, sp)
            if not total or total < 1 then total = 1 end
            return total
        end
        return math.max(1, math.floor(hardness + 0.5))
    end

    local function DropMaterialToNeighbors(bIndex, material, count)
        if count <= 0 then return 0 end
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        if not Drops then return 0 end
        local icon
        local Transform = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransform
        if Transform and Transform.Get then
            local def = Transform:Get(material)
            if def and def.dropIcon then icon = def.dropIcon end
        end
        if not icon then
            local Data = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainData
            if Data and Data:Get(material) then icon = Data:Get(material).texture end
        end
        local neighbors = Grid:Neighbors(bIndex)
        if #neighbors == 0 then return 0 end
        local placed = 0
        for _ = 1, count do
            for _try = 1, #neighbors do
                local n = neighbors[math.random(#neighbors)]
                local c = Grid:GetCell(n)
                if c and c.terrain ~= "void" then
                    if Drops:Add(n, material) then
                        placed = placed + 1
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return placed
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- BUILD: порциями по 1 материалу.
    -- ========================================================================
    local function TickBuild(entity, ai, bIndex)
        local b = Grid:GetBuilding(bIndex)
        if not b then return true end

        local totalHP  = tonumber(b.max) or 0
        local curH     = Grid:GetHardness(bIndex) or tonumber(b.hp) or 0
        local material = b.material or "wood"
        local hpPer    = LadderParams(material)
        local stored   = Grid.GetStored and Grid:GetStored(bIndex) or 0

        if totalHP > 0 and curH >= totalHP then
            Grid:SetHardness(bIndex, totalHP)
            b.hp = totalHP
            ai.work = nil
            ai.wasBuilding = true
            local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi
            if T and T.RenderCellFromStorage then T:RenderCellFromStorage(bIndex) end
            dbg(string.format("TickBuild cell=%d: DONE hp=%d/%d", bIndex, totalHP, totalHP))
            return true
        end

        if stored <= 0 then
            ai.work = nil
            dbg(string.format("TickBuild cell=%d: stored=0 → need_material (hp=%d/%d)",
                bIndex, math.floor(curH + 0.5), totalHP))
            return false, "need_material"
        end

        local w = ai.work
        if not w or w.kind ~= "build" then
            local total = CalcClicks("builder", "agility", totalHP, entity)
            local stepDamage = (totalHP > 0) and (totalHP / total) or 0
            w = {
                kind             = "build",
                total            = total,
                stepDamage       = stepDamage,
                hpSinceMaterial  = 0,
            }
            ai.work = w
        end

        w.hpSinceMaterial = w.hpSinceMaterial + w.stepDamage
        local newH = curH + w.stepDamage
        if newH > totalHP then newH = totalHP end
        Grid:SetHardness(bIndex, newH)
        b.hp = newH
        if b.by == nil then b.by = entity.id end

        if w.hpSinceMaterial >= hpPer then
            Grid:TakeStored(bIndex, 1)
            w.hpSinceMaterial = w.hpSinceMaterial - hpPer
            stored = Grid:GetStored(bIndex)
            dbg(string.format("TickBuild cell=%d: материал израсходован, stored=%d (hp=%d/%d)",
                bIndex, stored, math.floor(newH + 0.5), totalHP))
        end

        local Stats = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIStats
        if Stats and Stats.TryUpStat then Stats:TryUpStat(entity, "agility") end

        if totalHP > 0 and newH >= totalHP then
            Grid:SetHardness(bIndex, totalHP)
            b.hp = totalHP
            ai.work = nil
            ai.wasBuilding = true
            local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi
            if T and T.RenderCellFromStorage then T:RenderCellFromStorage(bIndex) end
            dbg(string.format("TickBuild cell=%d: DONE hp=%d/%d", bIndex, totalHP, totalHP))
            return true
        end

        if Grid:GetStored(bIndex) <= 0 then
            ai.work = nil
            dbg(string.format("TickBuild cell=%d: stored кончился → need_material (hp=%d/%d)",
                bIndex, math.floor(newH + 0.5), totalHP))
            return false, "need_material"
        end

        return false
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- DEMOLISH
    -- ========================================================================
    local function TickDemolish(entity, ai, bIndex)
        local b = Grid:GetBuilding(bIndex)
        if not b then return true end
        if ai.carrying then ai.carrying = nil end

        local totalHP  = tonumber(b.max) or 0
        local curH     = Grid:GetHardness(bIndex) or tonumber(b.hp) or 0
        local material = b.material or "wood"
        local hpPer, returnPer = LadderParams(material)

        if curH <= 0 then
            Grid:SetHardness(bIndex, 0)
            b.hp = 0
            ai.work = nil
            ai.wasBuilding = true
            local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi
            if T and T.RenderCellFromStorage then T:RenderCellFromStorage(bIndex) end
            return true
        end

        local w = ai.work
        if not w or w.kind ~= "demolish" then
            local total = CalcClicks("builder", "agility", curH, entity)
            local stepDamage = (total > 0) and (curH / total) or 0
            w = { kind = "demolish", done = 0, total = total, startHardness = curH,
                  stepDamage = stepDamage, materialDropped = false }
            ai.work = w
        end

        w.done = w.done + 1
        local newH = curH - w.stepDamage
        if newH < 0 then newH = 0 end
        Grid:SetHardness(bIndex, newH)
        b.hp = newH
        if b.by == nil then b.by = entity.id end

        local Stats = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIStats
        if Stats and Stats.TryUpStat then Stats:TryUpStat(entity, "agility") end

        if newH <= 0 then
            if not w.materialDropped then
                local count = math.max(0, math.floor(totalHP / (returnPer or 100)))
                if count > 0 then DropMaterialToNeighbors(bIndex, material, count) end
                w.materialDropped = true
            end
            Grid:SetHardness(bIndex, 0)
            b.hp = 0
            ai.work = nil
            ai.wasBuilding = true
            local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi
            if T and T.RenderCellFromStorage then T:RenderCellFromStorage(bIndex) end
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- BREAK
    -- ========================================================================
    local function TickBreak(entity, ai)
        local curH = Grid:GetHardness(ai.target)
        if curH == nil or curH <= 0 then return true end
        local w = ai.work
        if not w or w.kind ~= "break" then
            local ProfApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfProfessionsApi
            local profDef = ai.profId and ProfApi and ProfApi:Get(ai.profId) or nil
            local total = CalcClicks(ai.profId, profDef and profDef.stat, curH, entity)
            local stepDamage = curH / total
            w = { kind = "break", done = 0, total = total, startHardness = curH, stepDamage = stepDamage }
            ai.work = w
        end
        w.done = w.done + 1
        local hardness = Grid:GetHardness(ai.target) or 0
        local newHardness = hardness - w.stepDamage
        if newHardness < 0 then newHardness = 0 end
        Grid:SetHardness(ai.target, newHardness)
        local Stats = NSQC4.QuestsDfAIStats
        if Stats then
            local rule = TERRAIN_TO_STAT[ai.terrainId]
            if rule and rule.stat then Stats:TryUpStat(entity, rule.stat) end
            local wantVitality = (rule == nil) or (rule.vitality ~= false)
            if wantVitality then Stats:TryUpVitality(entity) end
        end
        if newHardness <= 0 then
            Grid:SetHardness(ai.target, 0)
            return true
        end
        return false
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Tick
    -- ========================================================================
    function Work:Tick(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return nil end
        local ai = entity.opts.ai
        if not ai or ai.state ~= "working" then return nil end
        if not ai.target then return nil end

        local bIndex = ai.workTarget or ai.target

        if Grid.IsBuilding and Grid:IsBuilding(bIndex) then
            local b = Grid:GetBuilding(bIndex)
            local hp  = tonumber(b.hp)  or 0
            local max = tonumber(b.max) or 0
            if hp < max then
                return TickBuild(entity, ai, bIndex)
            else
                return TickDemolish(entity, ai, bIndex)
            end
        end

        return TickBreak(entity, ai)
    end

    function Work:Reset(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return end
        local ai = entity.opts.ai
        if ai then ai.work = nil; ai.buildWork = nil end
    end

    SLASH_NSWORK1 = "/ns_work"
    SlashCmdList["NSWORK"] = function(msg)
        local cmd, arg = (msg or ""):match("^(%S*)%s*(.-)$")
        cmd = (cmd or ""):lower()
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then Work.DEBUG = true; print("[WORK] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then Work.DEBUG = false; print("[WORK] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "reset" then
            local E = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            local e = arg ~= "" and E and E:GetByName(arg) or nil
            if e and e.opts.ai then e.opts.ai.work = nil; e.opts.ai.state = "idle"; print("[WORK] reset " .. tostring(arg)) end
        else print("[WORK] /ns_work on | off | reset <name>") end
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Actions/Terrain/Earth/Check.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Earth / Check
-- Проверка: earth в клетке?
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local A = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
    if not A then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Earth/Check: Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    A.Types["earth"] = A.Types["earth"] or {}

    A.Types["earth"].Check = function(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 then return false end

        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return false end

        return Grid:GetTerrain(index) == "earth"
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Actions/Terrain/Earth/LeftClickEntities.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Earth / LeftClickEntities
-- Действие ЛКМ по существу в клетке с "earth".
-- Вызывается из LeftClickSelf.lua, когда существо НЕ спит.
--
-- Разворачивает/сворачивает существо. Если идёт анимация движения —
-- Animate:SetExpanded (мгновенно меняет размер temp), иначе Spawn
-- (перерисовывает anchor в клетке).
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local A = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
    if not A then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Earth/LeftClickEntities: Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    A.Types["earth"] = A.Types["earth"] or {}

    A.Types["earth"].LeftClickEntities = function(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 then return false end

        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return false end

        local entity = Grid:GetEntity(index)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end

        if entity.opts.sleeping then return false end

        entity.opts.expanded = not entity.opts.expanded

        local Animate = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntitiesAnimate
        if Animate and Animate:IsAnimating(entity.id) then
            Animate:SetExpanded(entity.id, entity.opts.expanded)
        else
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if Entities then
                Entities:Spawn(entity.id, index, entity.opts)
            end
        end

        print(string.format("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r %s: %s",
            entity.opts.name or "Существо",
            entity.opts.expanded and "развёрнут" or "свёрнут"))

        return true
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Actions/Terrain/Earth/LeftClickSelf.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Earth / LeftClickSelf
-- ЛКМ по earth:
--   1. Есть активное (не спящее) существо → передаём существу (LeftClickEntities)
--   2. Нет существа / спит:
--      - есть лут → ничего не делаем (не выделяем)
--      - нет лута → toggle выделения ландшафта
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local A = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
    if not A then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Earth/LeftClickSelf: Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local QuestsField = NSQC4.QuestsField
    if not QuestsField then return end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid

    A.Types["earth"] = A.Types["earth"] or {}

    A.Types["earth"].LeftClickSelf = function(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 then return false end

        QuestsField:SetCellOnMouseClick(index, "LeftButton", function(btn, button, idx)
            local Drops  = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
            local entity = Grid and Grid:GetEntity(idx)
            local sleeping = entity and entity.opts and entity.opts.sleeping

            -- 1. Активное существо → ему
            if entity and not sleeping then
                local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
                          and NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions.Types
                          and NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions.Types["earth"]
                if T and T.LeftClickEntities then
                    return T.LeftClickEntities(idx)
                end
                return false
            end

            -- 2. Нет существа / спит → если лут, не выделяем
            local hasLoot = Drops and Drops:HasAny(idx) or false
            if hasLoot then
                return false
            end

            -- 3. Иначе — toggle выделения
            local cell = QuestsField.cells and QuestsField.cells[idx]
            if not Grid then return false end

            local isSelected = Grid:IsTerrainSelected(idx)

            if isSelected then
                Grid:SetTerrainSelected(idx, "earth", false)
                if cell and cell.texture then
                    cell.texture:SetVertexColor(1, 1, 1, 1)
                end
                print("|cffff8800[NSQC4]|r Снято выделение earth в клетке " .. idx)
            else
                Grid:SetTerrainSelected(idx, "earth", true)
                if cell and cell.texture then
                    cell.texture:SetVertexColor(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 1)
                end
                print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Выделено earth в клетке " .. idx)
            end

            return true
        end)

        return true
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Actions/Terrain/Earth/RightClickEntities.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Earth / RightClickEntities
-- ПКМ по earth.
--
-- ЛОГИКА:
--   1. Есть существо (активное ИЛИ спящее) → меню существа:
--        - «Спать» (если активно) / «Разбудить» (если спит)
--        - «Осмотреть»
--      Меню показываем даже если есть лут — иначе нельзя разбудить
--      существо на клетке с лутом.
--   2. Существа нет и есть лут → меню не показываем (return false).
--   3. Существа нет и лута нет → меню клетки: «Постройка бассейна».
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local A = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
    if not A then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Earth/RightClickEntities: Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local QuestsField = NSQC4.QuestsField
    if not QuestsField then return end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid

    A.Types["earth"] = A.Types["earth"] or {}

    A.Types["earth"].RightClickEntities = function(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 then return false end

        QuestsField:SetCellOnMouseClick(index, "RightButton", function(btn, button, idx)
            local Drops  = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
            local entity = Grid and Grid:GetEntity(idx)
            local hasEntity = entity ~= nil
            local sleeping  = entity and entity.opts and entity.opts.sleeping
            local hasLoot   = Drops and Drops:HasAny(idx) or false

            -- 1. Существо → меню существа
            if hasEntity and entity.opts then
                local opts = entity.opts
                local menuItems = {}

                if sleeping then
                    menuItems[#menuItems + 1] = {
                        text = "Разбудить",
                        func = function()
                            opts.sleeping = false
                            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
                            if Entities then
                                Entities:Spawn(entity.id, idx, opts)
                            end
                            print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r " ..
                                  (opts.name or "Существо") .. " проснулся.")
                        end,
                    }
                else
                    menuItems[#menuItems + 1] = {
                        text = "Спать",
                        func = function()
                            opts.sleeping = true
                            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
                            if Entities then
                                Entities:Spawn(entity.id, idx, opts)
                            end
                            print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r " ..
                                  (opts.name or "Существо") .. " уснул.")
                        end,
                    }
                end

                menuItems[#menuItems + 1] = {
                    text = "Осмотреть",
                    func = function()
                        print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Вы осмотрели " ..
                              (opts.name or "существо") .. ".")
                    end,
                }

                QuestsField:ShowContextMenu(menuItems, btn)
                return true
            end

            -- 2. Существа нет, есть лут → меню не показываем
            if hasLoot then
                return false
            end

            -- 3. Существа нет, лута нет → меню клетки
            local menuItems = {
                { text = "Постройка бассейна", func = function()
                    local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
                    if Terrain and Terrain.SetTerrain then
                        Terrain:SetTerrain(idx, "pool")
                    end
                    print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Бассейн построен (заглушка) в клетке " .. idx)
                end },
            }
            QuestsField:ShowContextMenu(menuItems, btn)
            return true
        end)

        return true
    end

end)

-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Grass / Check

        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Grass/Check: Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    A.Types["grass"] = A.Types["grass"] or {}

    A.Types["grass"].Check = function(index)

    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Actions/Terrain/Grass/LeftClickEntities.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Grass / LeftClickEntities
-- Действие ЛКМ по существу в клетке с "grass".
-- Вызывается из LeftClickSelf.lua, когда существо НЕ спит.
--
-- Разворачивает/сворачивает существо. Если идёт анимация — Animate:SetExpanded,
-- иначе Spawn.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local A = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
    if not A then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Grass/LeftClickEntities: Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    A.Types["grass"] = A.Types["grass"] or {}

    A.Types["grass"].LeftClickEntities = function(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 then return false end

        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return false end

        local entity = Grid:GetEntity(index)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return false end

        if entity.opts.sleeping then return false end

        entity.opts.expanded = not entity.opts.expanded

        local Animate = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntitiesAnimate
        if Animate and Animate:IsAnimating(entity.id) then
            Animate:SetExpanded(entity.id, entity.opts.expanded)
        else
            local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
            if Entities then
                Entities:Spawn(entity.id, index, entity.opts)
            end
        end

        print(string.format("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r %s: %s",
            entity.opts.name or "Существо",
            entity.opts.expanded and "развёрнут" or "свёрнут"))

        return true
    end

end)

-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Grass / LeftClickSelf

        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 then return false end

        QuestsField:SetCellOnMouseClick(index, "LeftButton", function(btn, button, idx)
            local Drops  = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
            local entity = Grid and Grid:GetEntity(idx)

                local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions

                end
                return false
            end

            local hasLoot = Drops and Drops:HasAny(idx) or false
            if hasLoot then
                return false
            end

            if isSelected then
                Grid:SetTerrainSelected(idx, "grass", false)
                if cell and cell.texture then
                    cell.texture:SetVertexColor(1, 1, 1, 1)
                end
                print("|cffff8800[NSQC4]|r Снято выделение grass в клетке " .. idx)
            else
                Grid:SetTerrainSelected(idx, "grass", true)
                if cell and cell.texture then
                    cell.texture:SetVertexColor(0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 1)
                end
                print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Выделено grass в клетке " .. idx)
            end

            return true
        end)

        return true
    end

end)

======================================================================
 FILE: df/Actions/Terrain/Init.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Actions / Terrain / Init
-- Каркас «объекта ландшафта» для регистрации кликов.
--
-- Namespace: NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
--
-- Подпапки (по типу ландшафта), каждая с четырьмя файлами:
--   Earth/  { Check, LeftClickSelf, LeftClickEntities, RightClickEntities }
--   Grass/  { Check, LeftClickSelf, LeftClickEntities, RightClickEntities }
--   Stone/  { Check, LeftClickSelf, LeftClickEntities, RightClickEntities }
--   Tree/   { Check, LeftClickSelf, LeftClickEntities, RightClickEntities }
--   Pool/   { Check, LeftClickSelf, LeftClickEntities, RightClickEntities }
--
-- Каждый тип регистрирует себя в реестре:
--   TerrainActions.Types[<id>] = {
--       Check, LeftClickSelf, LeftClickEntities, RightClickEntities
--   }
--
-- Точка входа: TerrainUIApi:RenderCellFromStorage(index) зовёт
--   local T = TerrainActions.Types[terrainId]
--   if T and T.Check and T.Check(index) then
--       T.LeftClickSelf(index)
--       T.RightClickEntities(index)
--   end
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Actions = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions = Actions

    -- Реестр типов: id → { Check, LeftClickSelf, LeftClickEntities, RightClickEntities }
    Actions.Types = {}

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Регистрация типа (вызывается из подпапки)
    --   id      — "earth" | "grass" | "stone" | "tree" | "pool"
    --   handler — { Check=fn, LeftClickSelf=fn, LeftClickEntities=fn, RightClickEntities=fn }
    -- ========================================================================
    function Actions:RegisterTypeHandler(id, handler)
        if type(id) ~= "string" or id == "" then return end
        if type(handler) ~= "table" then return end
        self.Types[id] = handler
    end

    function Actions:GetTypeHandler(id)
        return self.Types[id]
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Проверка готовности (ленивая, для диагностики)
    -- ========================================================================
    function Actions:Init()
        local count = 0
        for id, handler in pairs(self.Types) do
            if handler.Check
               and handler.LeftClickSelf
               and handler.LeftClickEntities
               and handler.RightClickEntities then
                count = count + 1
            else
                print("|cffffaa00[NSQC4]|r TerrainActions: тип '" .. id .. "' зарегистрирован неполно.")
            end
        end

        if count == 0 then
            print("|cffffaa00[NSQC4]|r TerrainActions: ни один тип ландшафта не зарегистрирован.")
            return false
        end

        return true
    end

end)
