======================================================================
 FILE: df/Terrain/Logic/Init.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Terrain / Logic / Init
-- Менеджер генерации и слэш-команд для ландшафта.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
    if not Terrain then return end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not CONST then return end
    local COLS, ROWS = CONST.COLS, CONST.ROWS
    local TOTAL = COLS * ROWS

    -- ========================================================================
    -- УСТАНОВКА ТИПА
    -- ========================================================================
    function Terrain:SetTerrain(index, terrainId, opts)
        -- Просто вызываем SetAt, типы уже зарегистрированы в Storage
        return self:SetAt(index, terrainId, opts)
    end

    function Terrain:ClearTerrain(index)
        return self:ClearAt(index)
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- ГЕНЕРАЦИЯ (Пятна и случайное заполнение)
    -- ========================================================================
    function Terrain:SeedDemo(kind, count)
        kind = kind or "grass"
        count = math.min(tonumber(count) or 20, TOTAL)

        local free = {}
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            if not self:GetAt(i) then
                free[#free + 1] = i
            end
        end

        if #free == 0 then return 0 end

        local made = 0
        for _ = 1, count do
            if #free == 0 then break end
            local pick = math.random(#free)
            local idx = table.remove(free, pick)
            self:SetTerrain(idx, kind)
            made = made + 1
        end

        if self.Render then self:Render() end
        return made
    end

    function Terrain:SeedPatch(kind, centerIndex, radius)
        kind = kind or "grass"
        centerIndex = tonumber(centerIndex) or 0
        radius = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(radius) or 1))

        if centerIndex < 1 or centerIndex > TOTAL then return 0 end

        local function RC(index)
            local i = index - 1
            return i % COLS, math.floor(i / COLS)
        end

        local cCol, cRow = RC(centerIndex)
        local made = 0

        for row = cRow - radius, cRow + radius do
            for col = cCol - radius, cCol + radius do
                if col >= 0 and col < COLS and row >= 0 and row < ROWS then
                    local idx = row * COLS + col + 1
                    self:SetTerrain(idx, kind)
                    made = made + 1
                end
            end
        end

        if self.Render then self:Render() end
        return made
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- СЛЭШ-КОМАНДЫ
    -- ========================================================================
    SLASH_NSDF_TERRAIN_LOGIC1 = "/ns_df_terr"
    SlashCmdList["NSDF_TERRAIN_LOGIC"] = function(msg)
        local T = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
        if not T then return end

        local cmd, a1, a2, a3 = (msg or ""):match("^(%S*)%s*(%S*)%s*(%S*)%s*(%S*)")
        cmd = (cmd or ""):lower()

        if cmd == "" or cmd == "help" then
            print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r df/terrain — логика")
            print("  /ns_df_terr types                — список типов")
            print("  /ns_df_terr set <index> <id>     — поставить тип")
            print("  /ns_df_terr clear <index>        — снять тип")
            print("  /ns_df_terr seed <id> [count]    — залить случайные")
            print("  /ns_df_terr patch <id> <center> [radius] — пятно")

        elseif cmd == "types" then
            local types = T:GetTypes() or {}
            local names = {}
            for id in pairs(types) do names[#names + 1] = id end
            table.sort(names)
            print("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r terrain types:", #names)
            for _, id in ipairs(names) do
                local o = types[id]
                print(string.format("  %s → %s", id, tostring(o and o.texture)))
            end

        elseif cmd == "set" then
            local index = tonumber(a1)
            local id = a2
            if index and id then T:SetTerrain(index, id) end

        elseif cmd == "clear" then
            local index = tonumber(a1)
            if index then T:ClearTerrain(index) end

        elseif cmd == "seed" then
            local id = a1 ~= "" and a1 or "grass"
            local n = tonumber(a2) or 20
            T:SeedDemo(id, n)

        elseif cmd == "patch" then
            local id = a1 ~= "" and a1 or "grass"
            local center = tonumber(a2)
            local radius = tonumber(a3) or 1
            if center then T:SeedPatch(id, center, radius) end
        end
    end

end)

======================================================================
 FILE: df/Terrain/Logic/Water.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Terrain / Logic / Water
-- Логика и генерация типа "Вода" (water).
--
-- Два вида генерации (каждый с шансом 50%):
--   1. Река — две клетки на РАЗНЫХ сторонах поля (10×10), НЕ в углах.
--      Между ними изгибающийся путь из клеток воды (4-связность).
--   2. Озеро — 1..5 клеток воды связным «пятном», НЕ на крайних клетках
--      (col 1..8, row 1..8).
--
-- Каждая клетка воды получает глубину 1..10 (Grid:SetDepth).
--
-- Поведение при кликах — в Actions/Terrain/Water/*. Здесь только:
--   - регистрация типа "water" (onClick/contextMenu = пусто)
--   - SeedRiver()  — река (50% шанс)
--   - SeedLake()   — озеро (50% шанс)
--
-- Глубина зажимается Grid:SetDepth в 1..10 (см. Grid/Depth.lua).
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
    if not Terrain then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Terrain/Logic/Water: Terrain не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Terrain/Logic/Water: Grid не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not CONST then return end
    local COLS, ROWS = CONST.COLS, CONST.ROWS
    local TOTAL = COLS * ROWS

    -- Шанс появления каждого типа (0..1)
    local RIVER_CHANCE = 0.5
    local LAKE_CHANCE  = 0.5

    -- ========================================================================
    -- РЕГИСТРАЦИЯ ТИПА
    -- ========================================================================
    Terrain:RegisterType("water", {
        onClick = function(index)
            -- клики — в Actions/Terrain/Water/*
        end,
        contextMenu = nil,   -- ПКМ пока пустой
    })

    -- ========================================================================
    -- ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНОЕ: индекс ↔ (col, row)
    --   col 0..COLS-1, row 0..ROWS-1
    -- ========================================================================
    local function RC(index)
        local i = index - 1
        return i % COLS, math.floor(i / COLS)
    end

    local function CR(col, row)
        return row * COLS + col + 1
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Угловая ли клетка (col,row)?
    -- ========================================================================
    local function IsCorner(col, row)
        local onColEdge = (col == 0) or (col == COLS - 1)
        local onRowEdge = (row == 0) or (row == ROWS - 1)
        return onColEdge and onRowEdge
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНОЕ: поставить воду + глубину в клетке
    -- ========================================================================
    local function SetWaterCell(index, depth)
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end
        Terrain:SetTerrain(index, "water")
        local d = depth or math.random(1, 10)
        if Grid and Grid.SetDepth then
            Grid:SetDepth(index, d)
        end
        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- СЛУЧАЙНАЯ КЛЕТКА НА СТОРОНЕ (не в углу)
    --
    --   top    (row = 0):        col 1..COLS-2
    --   bottom (row = ROWS-1):   col 1..COLS-2
    --   left   (col = 0):        row 1..ROWS-2
    --   right  (col = COLS-1):   row 1..ROWS-2
    -- ========================================================================
    local SIDES = { "top", "bottom", "left", "right" }

    local function RandomCellOnSide(side)
        if side == "top" then
            return CR(math.random(1, COLS - 2), 0)
        elseif side == "bottom" then
            return CR(math.random(1, COLS - 2), ROWS - 1)
        elseif side == "left" then
            return CR(0, math.random(1, ROWS - 2))
        elseif side == "right" then
            return CR(COLS - 1, math.random(1, ROWS - 2))
        end
        return nil
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Две РАЗНЫЕ стороны
    -- ========================================================================
    local function PickTwoDifferentSides()
        local a = SIDES[math.random(#SIDES)]
        local b = a
        while b == a do
            b = SIDES[math.random(#SIDES)]
        end
        return a, b
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Строит путь от (c1,r1) до (c2,r2) изгибом.
    -- Возвращает массив индексов клеток (включая старт и финиш).
    -- (Не меняется — старая логика.)
    -- ========================================================================
    local function BuildWindingPath(c1, r1, c2, r2)
        local path = {}
        local visited = {}
        local curCol, curRow = c1, r1

        local function push(col, row)
            local idx = CR(col, row)
            if not visited[idx] then
                visited[idx] = true
                path[#path + 1] = idx
            end
        end

        push(curCol, curRow)

        local maxSteps = COLS * ROWS * 2
        local steps = 0

        while steps < maxSteps do
            steps = steps + 1
            if curCol == c2 and curRow == r2 then break end

            local candidates = {}

            -- Приоритет — направление к финишу
            if curCol < c2 then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "right", 3 } end
            if curCol > c2 then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "left",  3 } end
            if curRow < r2 then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "down",  3 } end
            if curRow > r2 then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "up",    3 } end

            -- Перпендикулярные «изгибы» — меньший вес, но ненулевой
            if curCol > 0          then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "left",  1 } end
            if curCol < COLS - 1   then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "right", 1 } end
            if curRow > 0          then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "up",    1 } end
            if curRow < ROWS - 1   then candidates[#candidates + 1] = { "down",  1 } end

            if #candidates == 0 then break end

            -- Взвешенный случайный выбор
            local totalW = 0
            for _, c in ipairs(candidates) do totalW = totalW + c[2] end
            local roll = math.random() * totalW
            local acc, chosen = 0, nil
            for _, c in ipairs(candidates) do
                acc = acc + c[2]
                if roll <= acc then chosen = c[1]; break end
            end

            local nc, nr = curCol, curRow
            if chosen == "left"  then nc = nc - 1 end
            if chosen == "right" then nc = nc + 1 end
            if chosen == "up"    then nr = nr - 1 end
            if chosen == "down"  then nr = nr + 1 end

            if nc < 0 then nc = 0 end
            if nc > COLS - 1 then nc = COLS - 1 end
            if nr < 0 then nr = 0 end
            if nr > ROWS - 1 then nr = ROWS - 1 end

            if nc ~= curCol or nr ~= curRow then
                curCol, curRow = nc, nr
                push(curCol, curRow)
            end
        end

        -- На всякий случай: если не дошли до финиша — дотягиваем прямыми шагами
        if curCol ~= c2 or curRow ~= r2 then
            while curCol ~= c2 do
                curCol = curCol + (c2 > curCol and 1 or -1)
                push(curCol, curRow)
            end
            while curRow ~= r2 do
                curRow = curRow + (r2 > curRow and 1 or -1)
                push(curCol, curRow)
            end
        end

        return path
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- РЕКА
    --   - шанс RIVER_CHANCE (50%)
    --   - старт и финиш на РАЗНЫХ сторонах, НЕ в углах
    --   - путь изгибом между ними
    --   - если клетки пути выходят в угол (промежуточные) — это допустимо
    --     (ТЗ: «1 клетка реки не в углу, последняя не в углу» — про старт/финиш)
    -- ========================================================================
    function Terrain:SeedRiver(opts)
        opts = opts or {}

        -- шанс появления
        local chance = opts.chance
        if chance == nil then chance = RIVER_CHANCE end
        if math.random() > chance then
            print(string.format("|cff808080[NSQC4]|r Река: нет (шанс %.0f%%)", chance * 100))
            return 0
        end

        local sideA, sideB = PickTwoDifferentSides()
        local startIdx = RandomCellOnSide(sideA)
        local endIdx   = RandomCellOnSide(sideB)
        if not startIdx or not endIdx or startIdx == endIdx then
            return 0
        end

        -- проверка на всякий случай: старт/финиш не в углу
        local sc1, sr1 = RC(startIdx)
        local sc2, sr2 = RC(endIdx)
        if IsCorner(sc1, sr1) or IsCorner(sc2, sr2) then
            print("|cffffaa00[NSQC4]|r Река: старт/финиш попали в угол — пропуск.")
            return 0
        end

        local path = BuildWindingPath(sc1, sr1, sc2, sr2)

        local depthMin = opts.depthMin or 1
        local depthMax = opts.depthMax or 10
        if depthMin < 1 then depthMin = 1 end
        if depthMax > 10 then depthMax = 10 end
        if depthMax < depthMin then depthMax = depthMin end

        local made = 0
        for _, idx in ipairs(path) do
            local d = math.random(depthMin, depthMax)
            if SetWaterCell(idx, d) then made = made + 1 end
        end

        if Terrain.Render then Terrain:Render() end
        print(string.format("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Река: %s → %s, клеток воды: %d",
            tostring(sideA), tostring(sideB), made))
        return made
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- ОЗЕРО
    --   - шанс LAKE_CHANCE (50%)
    --   - 1..5 клеток воды связным «пятном»
    --   - только внутренние клетки: col 1..COLS-2, row 1..ROWS-2
    -- ========================================================================

    -- Внутренняя ли клетка (не крайняя)?
    local function IsInner(col, row)
        if col < 1 or col > COLS - 2 then return false end
        if row < 1 or row > ROWS - 2 then return false end
        return true
    end

    -- Соседи (4-связность) — локально
    local function Neighbors4(index)
        local col, row = RC(index)
        local result = {}
        if row > 0 then result[#result + 1] = CR(col, row - 1) end
        if row < ROWS - 1 then result[#result + 1] = CR(col, row + 1) end
        if col > 0 then result[#result + 1] = CR(col - 1, row) end
        if col < COLS - 1 then result[#result + 1] = CR(col + 1, row) end
        return result
    end

    -- Связное пятно: BFS от center до count клеток, ТОЛЬКО внутренние.
    local function BuildLakeCells(center, count)
        local cells = {}
        local visited = {}
        local queue = { center }
        local head = 1
        visited[center] = true

        while head <= #queue and #cells < count do
            local cur = queue[head]
            head = head + 1
            cells[#cells + 1] = cur

            local neigh = Neighbors4(cur)
            for i = #neigh, 2, -1 do
                local j = math.random(i)
                neigh[i], neigh[j] = neigh[j], neigh[i]
            end

            for _, n in ipairs(neigh) do
                if not visited[n] then
                    local nc, nr = RC(n)
                    if IsInner(nc, nr) and #cells + (#queue - head + 1) < count + 1 then
                        visited[n] = true
                        queue[#queue + 1] = n
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return cells
    end

    -- Список всех внутренних клеток
    local function CollectInnerCells()
        local list = {}
        for row = 1, ROWS - 2 do
            for col = 1, COLS - 2 do
                list[#list + 1] = CR(col, row)
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    -- Публичный API: озеро.
    function Terrain:SeedLake(opts)
        opts = opts or {}

        -- шанс появления
        local chance = opts.chance
        if chance == nil then chance = LAKE_CHANCE end
        if math.random() > chance then
            print(string.format("|cff808080[NSQC4]|r Озеро: нет (шанс %.0f%%)", chance * 100))
            return 0
        end

        local count = tonumber(opts.count) or math.random(1, 5)
        if count < 1 then count = 1 end
        if count > 5 then count = 5 end

        -- центр — только внутренняя клетка
        local inner = CollectInnerCells()
        if #inner == 0 then return 0 end

        local center = inner[math.random(#inner)]
        local cells = BuildLakeCells(center, count)

        local depthMin = opts.depthMin or 1
        local depthMax = opts.depthMax or 10
        if depthMin < 1 then depthMin = 1 end
        if depthMax > 10 then depthMax = 10 end
        if depthMax < depthMin then depthMax = depthMin end

        local made = 0
        for _, idx in ipairs(cells) do
            local d = math.random(depthMin, depthMax)
            if SetWaterCell(idx, d) then made = made + 1 end
        end

        if Terrain.Render then Terrain:Render() end
        print(string.format("|cff00ff00[NSQC4]|r Озеро: центр %d, клеток воды: %d",
            center, made))
        return made
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Terrain/Storage.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Terrain / Storage
-- Хранилище ландшафта: адаптер к общему Grid + точка входа рендера.
--
-- Для void  hardness = nil (не добывается), depth = nil.
-- Для water hardness = nil (не добывается), depth ставит генератор воды
--            (Terrain/Logic/Water.lua) через Grid:SetDepth — SetAt её не трогает.
-- Для остальных hardness берётся из TerrainData, depth = nil.
--
-- ФИКС: при смене terrain снимаем выделение со старого типа —
-- иначе в selections.terrain_types остаётся устаревшая запись, и
-- Find:Pick может выбрать клетку, которая уже стала void.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
    if not Terrain then return end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not CONST then return end
    local TOTAL = CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then return end

    local function UIApi()
        return NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi
    end

    local function TerrainData()
        return NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainData
    end

    local function RenderCell(index)
        local api = UIApi()
        if api and api.RenderCellFromStorage then
            api:RenderCellFromStorage(index)
        end
    end

    local function RenderAll()
        local api = UIApi()
        if api and api.RenderAll then
            api:RenderAll()
        end
    end

    local function ClearCellClicks(index)
        local QuestsField = NSQC4.QuestsField
        if not QuestsField then return end
        if QuestsField.ClearCellMouse then
            QuestsField:ClearCellMouse(index, "LeftButton")
            QuestsField:ClearCellMouse(index, "RightButton")
        end
    end

    local function EnsureTable()
        nsDbc4 = nsDbc4 or {}
        nsDbc4.quests = nsDbc4.quests or {}
        nsDbc4.quests.df = nsDbc4.quests.df or {}
        nsDbc4.quests.df.terrain = nsDbc4.quests.df.terrain or {}

        local s = nsDbc4.quests.df.terrain
        s.types = s.types or {}
        s.initialized = s.initialized and true or false
        return s
    end

    function Terrain:EnsureStorage() return EnsureTable() end
    function Terrain:IsInitialized() return EnsureTable().initialized and true or false end
    function Terrain:SetInitialized(v) EnsureTable().initialized = v and true or false end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Снять выделение со старого terrain в клетке (если было)
    -- ========================================================================
    local function ClearOldSelection(index, newTerrainId)
        if not Grid or not Grid.IsTerrainSelected then return end
        local isSel, oldType = Grid:IsTerrainSelected(index)
        if isSel and oldType and oldType ~= newTerrainId then
            Grid:SetTerrainSelected(index, oldType, false)
        end
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Реестр типов
    -- ========================================================================
    function Terrain:RegisterType(id, opts)
        if type(id) ~= "string" or id == "" then return nil end
        local s = EnsureTable()
        s.types[id] = opts or {}
        return s.types[id]
    end

    function Terrain:GetType(id)
        local s = EnsureTable()
        return s.types[id]
    end

    function Terrain:GetTypes()
        return EnsureTable().types
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Карта
    -- ========================================================================
    function Terrain:GetAt(index)
        return Grid:GetTerrain(index)
    end

    function Terrain:SetAt(index, terrainId, opts)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end
        if type(terrainId) ~= "string" or terrainId == "" then return false end

        -- ФИКС: снять выделение со старого terrain
        ClearOldSelection(index, terrainId)

        local s = EnsureTable()
        if opts and not s.types[terrainId] then
            s.types[terrainId] = opts
        end

        Grid:SetTerrain(index, terrainId)

        local Data = TerrainData()
        local def = Data and Data:Get(terrainId)

        if terrainId == "void" then
            -- void: ни hardness, ни depth, ни выделения
            Grid:ClearHardness(index)
            if Grid.ClearDepth then Grid:ClearDepth(index) end
            if Grid.ClearSelection then
                Grid:ClearSelection("terrain", index)
            end
        elseif terrainId == "water" then
            -- water: hardness не добывается, depth ставит генератор воды
            -- (Terrain/Logic/Water.lua → Grid:SetDepth). SetAt её не трогает —
            -- иначе при каждом SetAt глубина сбрасывалась бы.
            Grid:ClearHardness(index)
            -- depth НЕ трогаем: генератор воды уже поставил её (или поставит
            -- после SetAt — порядок: SetWaterCell → SetTerrain + SetDepth).
        elseif def and def.hardness then
            Grid:SetHardness(index, def.hardness)
            if Grid.ClearDepth then Grid:ClearDepth(index) end
        else
            Grid:ClearHardness(index)
            if Grid.ClearDepth then Grid:ClearDepth(index) end
        end

        RenderCell(index)
        return true
    end

    function Terrain:ClearAt(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end

        local current = Grid:GetTerrain(index)
        if not current then return false end

        ClearOldSelection(index, nil)

        Grid:SetTerrain(index, nil)
        Grid:ClearHardness(index)
        if Grid.ClearDepth then Grid:ClearDepth(index) end
        ClearCellClicks(index)
        RenderCell(index)
        return true
    end

    function Terrain:Clear()
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            ClearOldSelection(i, nil)
            Grid:SetTerrain(i, nil)
            Grid:ClearHardness(i)
            if Grid.ClearDepth then Grid:ClearDepth(i) end
            ClearCellClicks(i)
        end
        RenderAll()

        if Grid.ClearAllSelections then
            Grid:ClearAllSelections("terrain")
        end
    end

    function Terrain:Count()
        local count = 0
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            if Grid:GetTerrain(i) then count = count + 1 end
        end
        return count
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Выделение
    -- ========================================================================
    function Terrain:ToggleSelect(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end

        local terrainId = Grid:GetTerrain(index)
        if not terrainId then return false end
        if terrainId == "void" then return false end   -- пустоту не выделяем
        -- water выделяем (по ТЗ ЛКМ по воде выделяет, работа — заглушка)

        local isSelected = Grid:IsTerrainSelected(index)

        if isSelected then
            Grid:SetTerrainSelected(index, terrainId, false)
        else
            Grid:SetTerrainSelected(index, terrainId, true)
        end

        RenderCell(index)
        return true
    end

    function Terrain:IsSelected(index)
        return Grid:IsTerrainSelected(index)
    end

    function Terrain:ClearSelection()
        Grid:ClearAllSelections("terrain")
        RenderAll()
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Рендер-вход
    -- ========================================================================
    function Terrain:RenderCell(index)
        RenderCell(index)
    end

    function Terrain:Render()
        RenderAll()
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Terrain/UI/Api.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Terrain / UI / Api
-- Порядок: ландшафт → строение → текст → ЛУТ → СУЩЕСТВО.
--
-- ФИКС: при рендере применяется HIGHLIGHT_SELECTED к текстуре клетки,
-- если Grid:IsTerrainSelected(index). Раньше выделение ставилось в
-- Grid, но не перекрашивало текстуру — «выделений нет».
--
-- ФИКС: T.LeftClickSelf / T.RightClickEntities зовутся только при
-- СМЕНЕ состояния клетки (_lastStateKey). Иначе SetCellOnMouseClick
-- перевешивается каждый рендер.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local QuestsField = NSQC4.QuestsField
    if not QuestsField then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Terrain/UI/Api: ядро поля не загружено.")
        return
    end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    if not CONST then return end
    local TOTAL = CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS

    local UIApi = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi = UIApi
    UIApi.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if UIApi.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[TUIA]|r", ...) end
    end

    local HIGHLIGHT_SELECTED = { 0.7, 0.56, 0.0, 1.0 }
    local DEFAULT_BG = { 0, 0, 0, 1 }

    local MATERIAL_TINT = {
        wood  = { 0.55, 0.35, 0.20, 1 },
        stone = { 0.60, 0.60, 0.60, 1 },
        metal = { 0.80, 0.80, 0.90, 1 },
        gold  = { 1.00, 0.85, 0.10, 1 },
    }

    local function ApplyTint(index, rgb)
        local cell = QuestsField.cells and QuestsField.cells[index]
        if not cell or not cell.texture then return end
        if rgb then
            cell.texture:SetVertexColor(rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3], rgb[4] or 1)
        else
            cell.texture:SetVertexColor(1, 1, 1, 1)
        end
    end

    local function ResolveTooltip(entity)
        if not entity or not entity.opts then return nil end
        local Entities = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
        local kind = entity.kind or entity.opts.kind
        if Entities and Entities.GetTooltip and kind then
            local builder = Entities:GetTooltip(kind)
            if builder then
                local ok, fn = pcall(builder, entity.opts)
                if ok and type(fn) == "function" then return fn end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function BuildingOwnerName(b)
        if not b or not b.by then return nil end
        if b.byName then return b.byName end
        local E = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntities
        if E and E.Get then
            local e = E:Get(b.by)
            if e and e.opts and e.opts.name then return e.opts.name end
        end
        return tostring(b.by)
    end

    local LOOT_NAMES = { earth="Земля", wood="Дерево", stone="Камень", herb="Трава" }

    local function LootLines(index)
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        if not Drops then return nil end
        local all = Drops:GetAll(index)
        if not all or #all == 0 then return nil end
        local counts, order = {}, {}
        for _, d in ipairs(all) do
            local id = d.id or "?"
            if not counts[id] then counts[id] = 0; order[#order+1] = id end
            counts[id] = counts[id] + 1
        end
        local parts = {}
        for _, id in ipairs(order) do
            parts[#parts+1] = string.format("%s ×%d", LOOT_NAMES[id] or id, counts[id])
        end
        return table.concat(parts, ", ")
    end

    local function CellLines(index, terrainId)
        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        local Data = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainData
        if not Grid then return nil end
        local lines = {}

        local tname = terrainId or "?"
        local tcolor = { 0.85, 0.85, 0.85 }
        if Data then
            local def = terrainId and Data:Get(terrainId)
            if def and def.color then tcolor = def.color end
        end
        lines[#lines+1] = { text = "Клетка: " .. tname, r = tcolor[1] or 0.85, g = tcolor[2] or 0.85, b = tcolor[3] or 0.85 }

        if terrainId == "water" then
            local d = Grid.GetDepth and Grid:GetDepth(index)
            if d then lines[#lines+1] = { text = string.format("Глубина: %d / %d", d, Grid.DEPTH_MAX or 10), r = 0.55, g = 0.75, b = 1.00 } end
        end

        if Grid.IsBuilding and Grid:IsBuilding(index) then
            local b = Grid:GetBuilding(index)
            if b then
                local hp  = tonumber(b.hp)  or 0
                local max = tonumber(b.max) or 1000
                local pct = (max > 0) and (hp / max * 100) or 0
                lines[#lines+1] = { text = string.format("Постройка: %d / %d (%.0f%%)", math.floor(hp+0.5), math.floor(max+0.5), pct), r = 0.55, g = 1.00, b = 0.55 }
                if b.material then
                    lines[#lines+1] = { text = "Материал: " .. tostring(b.material), r = 0.80, g = 0.80, b = 0.80 }
                end
                local ownerName = BuildingOwnerName(b)
                if ownerName then
                    lines[#lines+1] = { text = "Строит: " .. ownerName, r = 1.00, g = 0.82, b = 0.00 }
                end
            end
        else
            if terrainId and terrainId ~= "void" and terrainId ~= "water" then
                local curH = Grid:GetHardness(index)
                local maxH = nil
                if Data then
                    local def = Data:Get(terrainId)
                    maxH = def and def.hardness
                end
                if curH and maxH and maxH > 0 then
                    local pct = (curH / maxH) * 100
                    lines[#lines+1] = { text = string.format("Прочность: %d / %d (%.0f%%)", math.floor(curH+0.5), math.floor(maxH+0.5), pct), r = 0.85, g = 0.85, b = 0.85 }
                end
            end
        end

        local loot = LootLines(index)
        if loot then lines[#lines+1] = { text = "Лут: " .. loot, r = 0.8, g = 1.0, b = 0.8 } end
        return lines
    end

    local function ShowCellTooltip(index, terrainId)
        local lines = CellLines(index, terrainId)
        if not lines or #lines == 0 then return end
        GameTooltip:AddLine(lines[1].text, lines[1].r, lines[1].g, lines[1].b)
        for i = 2, #lines do
            GameTooltip:AddLine(lines[i].text, lines[i].r, lines[i].g, lines[i].b)
        end
        GameTooltip:Show()
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- State key: чтобы не перевешивать клики каждый рендер.
    -- ========================================================================
    local function StateKey(terrainId, hasEntity, entityId, hasLoot, selected)
        return tostring(terrainId).."|"..
               tostring(hasEntity).."|"..
               tostring(entityId).."|"..
               tostring(hasLoot).."|"..
               tostring(selected)
    end

    function UIApi:RenderCellFromStorage(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end

        local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
        if not Terrain then return false end

        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        local Data = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainData

        local terrainId = Terrain:GetAt(index)
        local isBuilding = (terrainId == "ladder")
            and Grid and Grid.IsBuilding and Grid:IsBuilding(index)

        local alpha, buildingTint = nil, nil
        local material = "wood"
        if isBuilding then
            alpha = 0.5
            local b = Grid:GetBuilding(index)
            if b and b.material then
                material = b.material
                buildingTint = MATERIAL_TINT[material]
            end
        end

        local cell = QuestsField.cells and QuestsField.cells[index]

        -- ПОДЛОЖКА
        if cell and cell.bg then
            if terrainId == "ladder" then
                cell.bg:SetTexture("Interface\\Buttons\\WHITE8x8")
                cell.bg:SetVertexColor(DEFAULT_BG[1], DEFAULT_BG[2], DEFAULT_BG[3], DEFAULT_BG[4])
                cell.bg:Show()
            else
                cell.bg:Hide()
            end
        end

        -- ЛАНДШАФТ + СТРОЕНИЕ
        if not terrainId then
            QuestsField:SetCellTexture(index, nil)
            if QuestsField.SetCellBuild then QuestsField:SetCellBuild(index, nil) end
            QuestsField:ClearCellText(index)
            QuestsField:ClearAnchorTextures(index)
        else
            local data = Data and Data:Get(terrainId) or {}
            local logic = Terrain:GetType(terrainId) or {}

            QuestsField:SetCellTexture(index, data.texture or logic.texture, nil)

            if QuestsField.SetCellBuild then
                if terrainId == "ladder" then
                    QuestsField:SetCellBuild(index, data.texture or logic.texture, alpha)
                else
                    QuestsField:SetCellBuild(index, nil)
                end
            end

            if data.text and data.text ~= "" then
                local c = data.color or { 1, 1, 1 }
                QuestsField:SetCellText(index, data.text, nil, c[1], c[2], c[3], data.textSize)
            else
                QuestsField:ClearCellText(index)
            end

            if terrainId == "ladder" and cell and cell.build then
                if buildingTint then
                    cell.build:SetVertexColor(buildingTint[1], buildingTint[2], buildingTint[3], buildingTint[4] or 1)
                else
                    cell.build:SetVertexColor(1, 1, 1, 1)
                end
            end

            if terrainId == "void" then
                ApplyTint(index, { 0, 0, 0, 1 })
            end
        end

        -- ЛУТ
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        if Drops then Drops:Render(index) end

        -- СУЩЕСТВО
        local entity = Grid and Grid:GetEntity(index)
        local EntitiesApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfEntitiesUIApi
        if EntitiesApi and EntitiesApi.Render then
            if entity and entity.opts then
                EntitiesApi:Render(index, {
                    icon     = entity.opts.icon or entity.opts.texture,
                    anchor   = entity.opts.anchor,
                    expanded = entity.opts.expanded,
                    alpha    = entity.opts.alpha,
                })
            else
                EntitiesApi:Render(index, nil)
            end
        end

        -- ФИКС: выделение — перекрасить текстуру
        local isSel = Grid and Grid:IsTerrainSelected(index)
        if isSel then
            ApplyTint(index, HIGHLIGHT_SELECTED)
        else
            ApplyTint(index, nil)
        end

        -- ТУЛТИП
        local sleeping   = entity and entity.opts and entity.opts.sleeping
        local hasEntity  = entity ~= nil
        local hasLoot    = Drops and Drops:HasAny(index) or false

        if hasEntity then
            entity.opts._landscape = { index = index, terrainId = terrainId }
            local tooltipFn = ResolveTooltip(entity)
            if tooltipFn then
                QuestsField:SetCellTooltip(index, function(btn)
                    tooltipFn(btn)
                    local lines = CellLines(index, terrainId)
                    if lines and #lines > 0 then
                        GameTooltip:AddLine(" ")
                        for _, ln in ipairs(lines) do
                            GameTooltip:AddLine(ln.text, ln.r, ln.g, ln.b)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            else
                QuestsField:SetCellTooltip(index, function(btn) ShowCellTooltip(index, terrainId) end)
            end
        else
            QuestsField:SetCellTooltip(index, function(btn) ShowCellTooltip(index, terrainId) end)
        end

        if hasLoot then
            QuestsField:SetCellOnMouseClick(index, "LeftButton", nil)
            if not hasEntity then
                QuestsField:SetCellOnMouseClick(index, "RightButton", nil)
                return true
            end
        end

        -- ФИКС: клики — только при смене состояния.
        local Actions = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainActions
        local T = Actions and Actions.Types and terrainId and Actions.Types[terrainId]
        local entityId = entity and entity.id or "nil"
        local key = StateKey(terrainId, hasEntity, entityId, hasLoot, isSel)

        if cell and cell._lastStateKey ~= key then
            cell._lastStateKey = key

            if T and T.Check and T.Check(index) then
                if T.LeftClickSelf then T.LeftClickSelf(index) end
                if T.RightClickEntities then T.RightClickEntities(index) end
            else
                if not hasEntity then
                    QuestsField:SetCellOnMouseClick(index, "LeftButton",  nil)
                    QuestsField:SetCellOnMouseClick(index, "RightButton", nil)
                end
            end
        end

        return true
    end

    function UIApi:RenderAll()
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            self:RenderCellFromStorage(i)
        end
    end

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Transform/Data.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Transform / Data
-- Таблица преобразований и лута по типам ландшафта.
--
-- def:
--   drop        — id дропа ("earth"|"wood"|"stone"|"herb"|nil)
--   dropIcon    — иконка дропа
--   dropMin     — минимум дропов
--   dropMax     — максимум дропов
--   dropTarget  — "self" | "neighbors"
--                 (если nextTerrain == "void", всегда "neighbors")
--   nextTerrain — во что превращается клетка ("earth"|"void")
--   eject       — вытолкнуть существо (для void — да)
--
-- Лут кладётся в лут-якоря (все 9, кроме TOPLEFT).
-- Если свободных лут-якорей нет — дроп не кладётся.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Transform = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransform
    if not Transform then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Transform/Data: Init.lua не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local IMG = "Interface\\AddOns\\NSQC4\\Media\\Images\\"

    -- tree → 1 wood в эту клетку, клетка → earth
    Transform:Register("tree", {
        drop        = "wood",
        dropIcon    = IMG .. "00t",
        dropMin     = 1,
        dropMax     = 1,
        dropTarget  = "self",
        nextTerrain = "earth",
        eject       = false,
    })

    -- stone → 2..5 stone в эту клетку, клетка → earth
    Transform:Register("stone", {
        drop        = "stone",
        dropIcon    = IMG .. "0ka",
        dropMin     = 2,
        dropMax     = 5,
        dropTarget  = "self",
        nextTerrain = "earth",
        eject       = false,
    })

    -- grass → 1..3 herb в эту клетку, клетка → earth
    Transform:Register("grass", {
        drop        = "herb",
        dropIcon    = IMG .. "00f",
        dropMin     = 1,
        dropMax     = 3,
        dropTarget  = "self",
        nextTerrain = "earth",
        eject       = false,
    })

    -- earth → 1 earth в соседние (50/50 на каждого), клетка → void
    Transform:Register("earth", {
        drop        = "earth",
        dropIcon    = IMG .. "00z",
        dropMin     = 1,
        dropMax     = 1,
        dropTarget  = "neighbors",
        nextTerrain = "void",
        eject       = true,
    })

    -- pool → 1..2 stone в соседние, клетка → void
    Transform:Register("pool", {
        drop        = "stone",
        dropIcon    = IMG .. "0ka",
        dropMin     = 1,
        dropMax     = 2,
        dropTarget  = "neighbors",
        nextTerrain = "void",
        eject       = true,
    })

    -- ladder → 0..1 wood в соседние, клетка → void
    Transform:Register("ladder", {
        drop        = "wood",
        dropIcon    = IMG .. "00t",
        dropMin     = 0,
        dropMax     = 1,
        dropTarget  = "neighbors",
        nextTerrain = "void",
        eject       = false,
    })

    print("|cff00ffff[TRANSFORM]|r Transform/Data загружен (" .. #Transform:GetAll() .. " типов).")

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Transform/Drops.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Transform / Drops
-- Лут: слой OVERLAY.
--
-- ЕДИНОЕ ПРАВИЛО: у всех лут-якорей одна логика размера/слоя/позиции.
-- Текстура берётся по d.id из таблицы ICON_BY_ID (fallback → d.icon → nil).
--
-- ПОРЯДОК СЛОЁВ (все — текстуры на btn, один frame level btn+10):
--   sublevel 2 — бордер лута (4 тонкие текстуры)
--   sublevel 3 — иконка лута
--   sublevel 4 — anchor существа (Anchors.lua)
--   sublevel 5 — temp существа (Entities/UI/Api.lua)
--
-- РАНЬШЕ бордер лута был ФРЕЙМОМ btn+11 — выше любых текстур btn+10,
-- поэтому существа оказывались ПОД дропами. Теперь бордер — текстуры
-- с sublevel 2, и всё в одном frame level: лут < anchor < temp.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Drops = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops = Drops

    local QuestsField = NSQC4.QuestsField
    if not QuestsField then return end

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then return end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    local TOTAL = CONST and (CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS) or 100
    local ANCHOR_INSET = CONST and CONST.ANCHOR_INSET or 0

    local IMG = "Interface\\AddOns\\NSQC4\\Media\\Images\\"

    -- Единая таблица иконок по id дропа
    local ICON_BY_ID = {
        wood  = IMG .. "00t",
        stone = IMG .. "0ka",
        herb  = IMG .. "00f",
        earth = IMG .. "00z",
    }

    local LOOT_ANCHORS = {
        "TOPCENTER", "TOPRIGHT",
        "MIDLEFT",   "CENTER",    "MIDRIGHT",
        "BOTLEFT",   "BOTCENTER", "BOTRIGHT",
    }
    Drops.LOOT_ANCHORS = LOOT_ANCHORS

    local LOOT_LAYER    = "OVERLAY"
    local LOOT_SUBLEVEL = 3

    local BORDER_LAYER    = "OVERLAY"
    local BORDER_SUBLEVEL = 2      -- ниже лута(3), anchor(4), temp(5)
    local BORDER_SIZE     = 1

    Drops.DEBUG = false

    local function dbg(...)
        if Drops.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[DROPS]|r", ...) end
    end

    -- Единый резолвер иконки: ICON_BY_ID[id] → d.icon → nil
    local function ResolveIcon(d)
        if not d then return nil end
        if d.id and ICON_BY_ID[d.id] then return ICON_BY_ID[d.id] end
        if d.icon then return d.icon end
        return nil
    end

    local function EnsureLoot(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c then return nil end
        if not c.loot then c.loot = {} end
        return c.loot
    end

    function Drops:FreeAnchors(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c then return {} end
        if c.terrain == "void" then return {} end
        local loot = c.loot or {}
        local free = {}
        for _, a in ipairs(LOOT_ANCHORS) do
            if not loot[a] then free[#free + 1] = a end
        end
        return free
    end

    function Drops:HasAny(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c or not c.loot then return false end
        for _ in pairs(c.loot) do return true end
        return false
    end

    function Drops:Count(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c or not c.loot then return 0 end
        local n = 0
        for _ in pairs(c.loot) do n = n + 1 end
        return n
    end

    function Drops:CountAllById(id)
        if not id then return 0 end
        local n = 0
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            local c = Grid:GetCell(i)
            if c and c.loot then
                for _, d in pairs(c.loot) do
                    if d.id == id then n = n + 1 end
                end
            end
        end
        return n
    end

    function Drops:TakeFromAnywhere(id)
        if not id then return false end
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            local c = Grid:GetCell(i)
            if c and c.loot then
                for anchor, d in pairs(c.loot) do
                    if d.id == id then
                        c.loot[anchor] = nil
                        local anyLeft = false
                        for _ in pairs(c.loot) do anyLeft = true; break end
                        if not anyLeft then c.loot = nil end
                        self:Render(i)
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    -- Add теперь принимает только id. Иконка — из ICON_BY_ID.
    function Drops:Add(index, id)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return nil end
        if not id then return nil end
        local free = self:FreeAnchors(index)
        if #free == 0 then return nil end
        local anchor = free[1]
        local loot = EnsureLoot(index)
        if not loot then return nil end
        loot[anchor] = { id = id }
        self:Render(index)
        return anchor
    end

    function Drops:Get(index, anchor)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c or not c.loot then return nil end
        return c.loot[anchor]
    end

    function Drops:GetAll(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c or not c.loot then return {} end
        local list = {}
        for _, a in ipairs(LOOT_ANCHORS) do
            local d = c.loot[a]
            if d then
                list[#list + 1] = { anchor = a, id = d.id }
            end
        end
        return list
    end

    function Drops:Clear(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c then return false end
        c.loot = nil
        self:Render(index)
        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Очистка текстур лута и его бордеров
    -- ========================================================================
    local function ClearLootBorders(btn)
        if not btn._lootBorders then return end
        for _, sides in pairs(btn._lootBorders) do
            for _, tex in ipairs(sides) do
                tex:Hide()
                tex:ClearAllPoints()
                tex:SetTexture(nil)
            end
        end
        btn._lootBorders = nil
    end

    local function ClearLootTextures(index)
        local btn = QuestsField.cells and QuestsField.cells[index]
        if not btn then return end
        if btn._lootTextures then
            for _, tex in pairs(btn._lootTextures) do
                tex:Hide()
                tex:ClearAllPoints()
                tex:SetTexture(nil)
            end
            btn._lootTextures = nil
        end
        ClearLootBorders(btn)
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Бордер лута: 4 тонкие текстуры на btn с sublevel 2.
    -- ========================================================================
    local function SetLootBorder(index, anchor, lootTex)
        local btn = QuestsField.cells and QuestsField.cells[index]
        if not btn or not lootTex then return end
        if not btn._lootBorders then btn._lootBorders = {} end
        local sides = btn._lootBorders[anchor]
        if not sides then
            sides = {}
            for i = 1, 4 do
                local t = btn:CreateTexture(nil, BORDER_LAYER, nil, BORDER_SUBLEVEL)
                t:SetTexture("Interface\\Buttons\\WHITE8x8")
                t:SetVertexColor(0, 0, 0, 1)
                sides[i] = t
            end
            btn._lootBorders[anchor] = sides
        end

        local top, bottom, left, right = sides[1], sides[2], sides[3], sides[4]

        top:ClearAllPoints()
        top:SetPoint("TOPLEFT",  lootTex, "TOPLEFT",  -BORDER_SIZE,  BORDER_SIZE)
        top:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", lootTex, "TOPRIGHT",  BORDER_SIZE,  BORDER_SIZE)
        top:SetHeight(BORDER_SIZE)
        top:Show()

        bottom:ClearAllPoints()
        bottom:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT",  lootTex, "BOTTOMLEFT",  -BORDER_SIZE, -BORDER_SIZE)
        bottom:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", lootTex, "BOTTOMRIGHT",  BORDER_SIZE, -BORDER_SIZE)
        bottom:SetHeight(BORDER_SIZE)
        bottom:Show()

        left:ClearAllPoints()
        left:SetPoint("TOPLEFT",    lootTex, "TOPLEFT",    -BORDER_SIZE, -BORDER_SIZE)
        left:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", lootTex, "BOTTOMLEFT", -BORDER_SIZE,  BORDER_SIZE)
        left:SetWidth(BORDER_SIZE)
        left:Show()

        right:ClearAllPoints()
        right:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT",    lootTex, "TOPRIGHT",    BORDER_SIZE, -BORDER_SIZE)
        right:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", lootTex, "BOTTOMRIGHT", BORDER_SIZE,  BORDER_SIZE)
        right:SetWidth(BORDER_SIZE)
        right:Show()
    end

    -- Единое правило позиции для всех анкоров
    local function AnchorPoint(tex, btn, anchor)
        local ins = ANCHOR_INSET
        if anchor == "TOPCENTER" then
            tex:SetPoint("TOP", btn, "TOP", 0, -ins)
        elseif anchor == "TOPRIGHT" then
            tex:SetPoint("TOPRIGHT", btn, "TOPRIGHT", -ins, -ins)
        elseif anchor == "MIDLEFT" then
            tex:SetPoint("LEFT", btn, "LEFT", ins, 0)
        elseif anchor == "CENTER" then
            tex:SetPoint("CENTER", btn, "CENTER", 0, 0)
        elseif anchor == "MIDRIGHT" then
            tex:SetPoint("RIGHT", btn, "RIGHT", -ins, 0)
        elseif anchor == "BOTLEFT" then
            tex:SetPoint("BOTTOMLEFT", btn, "BOTTOMLEFT", ins, ins)
        elseif anchor == "BOTCENTER" then
            tex:SetPoint("BOTTOM", btn, "BOTTOM", 0, ins)
        elseif anchor == "BOTRIGHT" then
            tex:SetPoint("BOTTOMRIGHT", btn, "BOTTOMRIGHT", -ins, ins)
        end
    end

    function Drops:Render(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end
        local btn = QuestsField.cells and QuestsField.cells[index]
        if not btn then return false end
        ClearLootTextures(index)
        local c = Grid:GetCell(index)
        if not c or not c.loot then return true end
        btn._lootTextures = {}
        for anchor, d in pairs(c.loot) do
            local icon = ResolveIcon(d)
            local tex = btn:CreateTexture(nil, LOOT_LAYER, nil, LOOT_SUBLEVEL)
            tex:SetTexture(icon)
            tex:SetTexCoord(0, 1, 0, 1)
            local size = btn:GetWidth() / 3 - 2
            tex:SetSize(size, size)
            AnchorPoint(tex, btn, anchor)
            tex:Show()
            btn._lootTextures[anchor] = tex
            SetLootBorder(index, anchor, tex)
        end
        return true
    end

    function Drops:RefreshAllLootSizes()
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            local btn = QuestsField.cells and QuestsField.cells[i]
            if btn and btn._lootTextures then
                local size = btn:GetWidth() / 3 - 2
                for anchor, tex in pairs(btn._lootTextures) do
                    if tex:IsShown() then
                        tex:SetSize(size, size)
                        SetLootBorder(i, anchor, tex)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function Drops:RenderAll()
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            self:Render(i)
        end
    end

    SLASH_NSDROPS1 = "/ns_drops"
    SlashCmdList["NSDROPS"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then
            Drops.DEBUG = true
            print("[DROPS] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            Drops.DEBUG = false
            print("[DROPS] DEBUG = false")
        else
            print("[DROPS] /ns_drops on | off")
        end
    end

    print("|cff00ffff[DROPS]|r Drops загружен (бордер лута — текстуры sublevel 2, существа выше).")

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Transform/Init.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Transform / Init
-- Реестр преобразований + Apply (раздача лута, смена terrain, выталкивание).
--
-- Отладка:
--   /ns_loot on | off | dump | map
--
-- ФИКС: при nextTerrain == "void" снимаем выделение с клетки,
-- иначе Find:Pick может выбрать устаревшую клетку и существо уйдёт в void.
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Transform = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfTransform = Transform

    Transform.DEBUG = true

    local function dbg(...)
        if Transform.DEBUG then print("|cff00ffff[LOOT]|r", ...) end
    end

    local byTerrain = {}
    local order     = {}

    function Transform:Register(terrainId, def)
        if type(terrainId) ~= "string" or terrainId == "" then return false end
        if type(def) ~= "table" then return false end
        if not byTerrain[terrainId] then
            order[#order + 1] = terrainId
        end
        def.id = terrainId
        byTerrain[terrainId] = def
        return true
    end

    function Transform:Get(terrainId)
        if type(terrainId) ~= "string" or terrainId == "" then return nil end
        return byTerrain[terrainId]
    end

    function Transform:GetAll()
        local list = {}
        for _, id in ipairs(order) do
            if byTerrain[id] then list[#list + 1] = byTerrain[id] end
        end
        return list
    end

    local function ClearSelectionFor(index)
        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid or not Grid.IsTerrainSelected then return end
        local isSel, oldType = Grid:IsTerrainSelected(index)
        if isSel and oldType then
            Grid:SetTerrainSelected(index, oldType, false)
        end
    end

    local function DropSelf(index, def)
        if not def.drop or not def.dropIcon then return 0 end
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        if not Drops then return 0 end
        local minN = tonumber(def.dropMin) or 1
        local maxN = tonumber(def.dropMax) or minN
        if minN < 0 then minN = 0 end
        if maxN < minN then maxN = minN end
        local n = math.random(minN, maxN)
        local placed = 0
        for _ = 1, n do
            local a = Drops:Add(index, def.drop, def.dropIcon)
            if not a then
                dbg(string.format("  self: cell %d — нет свободного якоря, дроп %s пропал", index, tostring(def.drop)))
                break
            end
            placed = placed + 1
            dbg(string.format("  self: cell %d anchor %s ← %s", index, tostring(a), tostring(def.drop)))
        end
        return placed
    end

    local function DropNeighbors(index, def)
        if not def.drop or not def.dropIcon then return 0 end
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        local Grid  = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Drops or not Grid then return 0 end

        local minN = tonumber(def.dropMin) or 1
        local maxN = tonumber(def.dropMax) or minN
        if minN < 0 then minN = 0 end
        if maxN < minN then maxN = minN end
        local total = math.random(minN, maxN)
        if total <= 0 then
            dbg(string.format("  neighbors: cell %d — дропов 0 (min=%d,max=%d)", index, minN, maxN))
            return 0
        end

        local neighbors = Grid:Neighbors(index)
        local placed = 0

        for i = 1, total do
            local shuffled = {}
            for j = 1, #neighbors do shuffled[j] = neighbors[j] end
            for j = #shuffled, 2, -1 do
                local k = math.random(j)
                shuffled[j], shuffled[k] = shuffled[k], shuffled[j]
            end

            local chosen = nil
            local tries = {}
            for _, n in ipairs(shuffled) do
                local roll = math.random()
                local ok = (roll < 0.5)
                tries[#tries + 1] = string.format("%d:%s(%.2f)", n, ok and "Y" or "n", roll)
                if ok then
                    local free = Drops:FreeAnchors(n)
                    if #free > 0 then
                        chosen = n
                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if chosen then
                local a = Drops:Add(chosen, def.drop, def.dropIcon)
                if a then
                    placed = placed + 1
                    dbg(string.format("  neighbors: drop %d/%d cell %d anchor %s ← %s  [%s]",
                        i, total, chosen, tostring(a), tostring(def.drop), table.concat(tries, " ")))
                else
                    dbg(string.format("  neighbors: drop %d/%d cell %d — Add вернул nil  [%s]",
                        i, total, chosen, table.concat(tries, " ")))
                end
            else
                dbg(string.format("  neighbors: drop %d/%d — никто не подошёл  [%s]",
                    i, total, table.concat(tries, " ")))
            end
        end
        return placed
    end

    local function EjectEntity(index)
        local Void = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformVoid
        if Void and Void.EjectEntity then
            local ok = Void:EjectEntity(index)
            dbg(string.format("  eject: cell %d → %s", index, ok and "ok" or "некуда"))
            return ok
        end

        local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
        if not Grid then return false end
        local entity = Grid:GetEntity(index)
        if not entity then return false end

        local neighbors = Grid:Neighbors(index)
        local candidates = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(neighbors) do
            local c = Grid:GetCell(n)
            if c and c.terrain ~= "void" and not c.entityName then
                candidates[#candidates + 1] = n
            end
        end
        if #candidates == 0 then return false end

        local target = candidates[math.random(#candidates)]
        Grid:SetEntityName(index, nil)
        Grid:SetEntityName(target, entity.opts and entity.opts.name)
        entity.index = target
        dbg(string.format("  eject: cell %d → cell %d", index, target))
        return true
    end

    function Transform:Apply(index, terrainId)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 then return false end

        local def = self:Get(terrainId)
        if not def then
            dbg(string.format("Apply: cell %d terrain=%s — нет def, пропуск", index, tostring(terrainId)))
            return false
        end

        local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
        if not Terrain then return false end

        dbg(string.format("Apply: cell %d terrain=%s next=%s drop=%s min=%s max=%s target=%s",
            index, tostring(terrainId), tostring(def.nextTerrain), tostring(def.drop),
            tostring(def.dropMin), tostring(def.dropMax), tostring(def.dropTarget)))

        local isVoidNext = (def.nextTerrain == "void")
        local target = def.dropTarget or "self"
        if isVoidNext then target = "neighbors" end

        if def.drop then
            if target == "neighbors" then
                DropNeighbors(index, def)
            else
                DropSelf(index, def)
            end
        end

        if def.nextTerrain then
            if isVoidNext then
                ClearSelectionFor(index)
            end
            Terrain:SetTerrain(index, def.nextTerrain)
            dbg(string.format("  terrain: cell %d → %s", index, tostring(def.nextTerrain)))
        end

        if isVoidNext or def.eject then
            EjectEntity(index)
        end

        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Отладочные команды
    -- ========================================================================
    local function DumpLoot()
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
        if not Drops or not Terrain then
            print("[LOOT] dump: Drops/Terrain не загружены")
            return
        end
        print("[LOOT] dump — клетки с лутом:")
        local found = 0
        for i = 1, 100 do
            if Drops:HasAny(i) then
                found = found + 1
                local t = Terrain:GetAt(i) or "?"
                local items = Drops:GetAll(i)
                local parts = {}
                for _, d in ipairs(items) do
                    parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("%s@%s", tostring(d.id), tostring(d.anchor))
                end
                print(string.format("  cell %d (%s): %s", i, tostring(t), table.concat(parts, ", ")))
            end
        end
        if found == 0 then print("  (пусто)") else print(string.format("  всего: %d", found)) end
    end

    local function DumpMap()
        local Drops = NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformDrops
        local Terrain = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrain
        if not Drops or not Terrain then return end
        local CH = { earth="E", grass="G", stone="S", tree="T", pool="P", void=".", ladder="L" }
        print("[LOOT] map:")
        for row = 9, 0, -1 do
            local line = ""
            for col = 0, 9 do
                local idx = row * 10 + col + 1
                local t = Terrain:GetAt(idx)
                local ch = CH[t or ""] or "?"
                local n = Drops:Count(idx)
                if n > 0 then line = line .. string.format("%s%d ", ch, n)
                else line = line .. ch .. "  " end
            end
            print("  " .. line)
        end
    end

    SLASH_NSLOOT1 = "/ns_loot"
    SlashCmdList["NSLOOT"] = function(msg)
        local cmd = (msg or ""):lower():match("^(%S*)")
        if cmd == "" or cmd == "on" then
            Transform.DEBUG = true
            print("[LOOT] DEBUG = true")
        elseif cmd == "off" then
            Transform.DEBUG = false
            print("[LOOT] DEBUG = false")
        elseif cmd == "dump" then
            DumpLoot()
        elseif cmd == "map" then
            DumpMap()
        else
            print("[LOOT] /ns_loot on | off | dump | map")
        end
    end

    print("|cff00ffff[TRANSFORM]|r Transform/Init загружен (DEBUG=" .. tostring(Transform.DEBUG) .. ")")

end)


======================================================================
 FILE: df/Transform/Void.lua
======================================================================

-- ============================================================================
-- NSQC4 / Quests / df / Transform / Void
-- Пустота: выталкивание существа, проверка, подсчёт.
--
-- Void:IsVoid(index)          → true, если клетка void
-- Void:EjectEntity(index)     → вытолкнуть существо в случайную соседнюю
--                               не-void клетку без существа
-- Void:CountVoid()            → сколько void-клеток на текущем этаже
-- Void:CountTotal()           → всего клеток (COLS*ROWS)
-- Void:PercentVoid()          → доля void (0..1) на текущем этаже
-- ============================================================================

NSQC4.RegisterModule("quests", function()

    local Void = {}
    NSQC4.QuestsDfTransformVoid = Void

    local Grid = NSQC4.QuestsDfGrid
    if not Grid then
        print("|cffff0000[NSQC4]|r Transform/Void: Grid не загружен.")
        return
    end

    local CONST = NSQC4.QuestsFieldConst
    local TOTAL = CONST and (CONST.COLS * CONST.ROWS) or 100

    -- ========================================================================
    -- IsVoid
    -- ========================================================================
    function Void:IsVoid(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end
        return Grid:GetTerrain(index) == "void"
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- EjectEntity: вытолкнуть существо из клетки index в случайную соседнюю
    -- не-void клетку без существа. Если соседей нет — существо остаётся.
    -- ========================================================================
    function Void:EjectEntity(index)
        index = tonumber(index) or 0
        if index < 1 or index > TOTAL then return false end

        local entity = Grid:GetEntity(index)
        if not entity then return false end

        local neighbors = Grid:Neighbors(index)
        local candidates = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(neighbors) do
            local c = Grid:GetCell(n)
            if c and c.terrain ~= "void" and not c.entityName then
                candidates[#candidates + 1] = n
            end
        end
        if #candidates == 0 then return false end

        local target = candidates[math.random(#candidates)]

        Grid:SetEntityName(index, nil)
        Grid:SetEntityName(target, entity.opts and entity.opts.name)
        entity.index = target

        -- перерисовать обе клетки
        local TerrainApi = NSQC4.QuestsDfTerrainUIApi
        if TerrainApi and TerrainApi.RenderCellFromStorage then
            TerrainApi:RenderCellFromStorage(index)
            TerrainApi:RenderCellFromStorage(target)
        end

        return true
    end

    -- ========================================================================
    -- Подсчёт void на текущем этаже
    -- ========================================================================
    function Void:CountVoid()
        local n = 0
        for i = 1, TOTAL do
            if Grid:GetTerrain(i) == "void" then
                n = n + 1
            end
        end
        return n
    end

    function Void:CountTotal()
        return TOTAL
    end

    function Void:PercentVoid()
        if TOTAL <= 0 then return 0 end
        return self:CountVoid() / TOTAL
    end

end)
